📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 更昔洛韦和阿昔洛韦显示出与 RSV 核蛋白的显着结合能和关键相互作用,ADMET 和 PASS 分析显示出作为 抗病毒药物 核蛋白药物的潜力。 [10.1002/bab.70193]。
  • 佐剂 RSVPreF3 的安全性与临床试验中观察到的一致,显示出未改变且有利的获益-风险特征。大多数不良事件是非严重的反应原事件,观察到的吉兰-巴利综合征和心房颤动的发生率并不表明与背景 发病率 相比风险过高。
  • 在第三次EQA中,73个实验室(90.1%)返回结果,其中69.9%为良好,15.1%为可接受,1.4%为满意(86.3%为理想),12.3%为不可接受,1.4%为差;在第四次EQA中,74个实验室(87.1%)返回结果,其中60.8%为良好,28.4%为可接受,2.7%为满意(91.9%为理想),8.1%为不可接受。
  • 与未感染 RSV-A Gdup+ 的儿童相比,RSV-A Gdup+ 感染的儿童 5 年当前哮喘的调整后比值比为 2.00 (95%CI = 1.15-3.47),而 RSV-B Gdup+ 感染的调整后比值比为 1.78 (95%CI = 1.01-3.11)。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • Ganciclovir and acyclovir revealed notable binding energy and critical interactions with RSV nucleoprotein, with ADMET and PASS analysis showing potential as antiviral drugs against nucleoprotein. [10.1002/bab.70193].
  • The safety profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 is consistent with that observed in clinical trials, showing an unchanged and favorable benefit-risk profile. [10.1007/s40264-026-01698-y].
  • In the third EQA, 73 laboratories (90.1%) returned results, with 69.9% classified as good, 15.1% acceptable, and 1.4% satisfactory (86.3% desirable), while 12.3% were unacceptable and 1.4% poor; in the fourth EQA, 74 laboratories (87.1%) returned results, with 60.8% good, 28.4% acceptable, and 2.7% satisfactory (91.9% desirable), and 8.1% unacceptable.
  • Children with RSV-A Gdup+ infections had an adjusted odds ratio of 2.00 (95%CI = 1.15-3.47) for 5-year current asthma compared to those without RSV infection, while RSV-B Gdup+ infections had an adjusted odds ratio of 1.78 (95%CI = 1.01-3.11).

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期呼吸道合胞病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • 关于 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的一般信息,呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是一种传染性病原体,会导致易感人群出现轻度感冒样疾病,但会导致严重的下呼吸道疾病。没有报告 暴发 或事件。 [news-b1c77daf01cc515e]。
  • 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是五岁以下儿童急性下呼吸道感染的主要原因,对全世界儿童健康构成持续威胁。婴儿早期感染 RSV 会显着增加患儿童哮喘的风险,尤其是有过敏或哮喘家族史的儿童。RSV 还会导致老年人出现严重后果和心脏并发症。
  • 辉瑞全球医疗补助金 (GMG) 与母胎医学协会 (SMFM) 合作发起的质量改进补助金机会,旨在改善孕产妇妊娠期间 RSV 免疫接种。 [news-7750abd7e0d25b7f]。
  • 根据 JCVI 的建议,已确认扩大 NHS RSV 疫苗接种 计划,将 65-74 岁的成年人纳入临床高危人群。 [news-a241a3f751031b21]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Respiratory syncytial virus news developments.
  • General information about respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), an infectious agent causing mild coldlike illness but severe lower respiratory disease in susceptible individuals. No outbreak or event is reported.
  • Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory infections in children under five and poses a persistent threat to child health worldwide. [news-bedfbee549edd3cf].
  • A Quality Improvement Grant opportunity launched by Pfizer Global Medical Grants (GMG) in collaboration with the Society for Maternal-Fetal Medicine (SMFM) to improve maternal RSV immunization delivery during pregnancy.
  • Confirmed expansion of NHS RSV vaccination programme to include adults aged 65-74 in clinical at-risk groups, advised by JCVI. [news-a241a3f751031b21].

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Influenza and other respiratory viruses  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-01  |  DOI: 10.1111/irv.70302  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 20, 8, e70302
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

世卫组织第三次和第四次 呼吸道合胞病毒 分子检测外部质量评估的结果。

Results From the Third and Fourth WHO External Quality Assessments for the Molecular Detection of Respiratory Syncytial Virus.
作者： Fernando do Couto Motta, Obadiah Kenji, Ian G. Barr, Shabana Bi, Anne von Gottberg, Jean-Michel Heraud, Siddhivinayak Hirve, Sanjiv Rughooputh, Nicole Wolter, Maria Zambon
摘要中文翻译摘要背景 2016年,世界卫生组织(WHO)启动了全球RSV 监测计划,旨在加强实验室诊断能力并评估分子检测测试性能。作为该计划的一部分,定期进行外部质量评估 (EQA),以确保持续的实验室熟练程度。进行了第三次(2021-2022年)和第四次(2023-2024年)WHO RSV EQA,以评估参与实验室准确检测 RSV 和分型的能力。方法 对于每个 EQA 组,使用最近流行的 RSV 菌株和之前 EQA 中使用的 RSV 菌株制备 12 种冻干 RSV 分离株。分离株分别被运送到第三次和第四次 EQA 的 81 个和 85 个实验室。参与者使用常规分子诊断方案测试样本,并通过英国 NEQAS 在线平台报告结果。根据检测和子类型准确度评估性能,并使用评分系统,范围从良好性能(24/24 正确结果)到不可接受(≤ 16 正确结果)。结果 共有 73 个 (90.1%) 和 74 个 (87.1%) 实验室分别返回了第三次和第四次 RSV 分子 EQA 的结果。在第三次EQA中,69.9%的实验室被评为良好,15.1%的实验室被评为合格,1.4%的实验室被评为合格,86.3%的实验室被评为合格;12.3%的人表示不可接受,1.4%的人表示较差。第四次EQA中,良好率为60.8%,合格率为28.4%,满意率为2.7%,合格率为91.9%;8.1%的人不可接受,没有一个是差的。结论 第三次和第四次 WHO RSV EQA 在 RSV 分子检测和分型方面表现出良好的实验室整体表现。这些发现强调了 EQA 对记录高质量诊断的重要性,特别是在引入 RSV 疫苗 和单克隆抗体后。正在进行的 EQA,包括 2024 年启动的首次 WHO RSV 测序 EQA,将进一步增强全球实验室应对 RSV 监测 和 暴发 的能力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
作为世卫组织于 2016 年启动的全球 RSV 呼吸道合胞病毒 计划的一部分,该研究需要加强实验室诊断能力并评估 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的分子检测测试性能。
设计与对象
外部质量评估(EQA)作为第三次(2021-2022年)和第四次(2023-2024年)世界卫生组织RSV EQA进行,评估参与实验室检测RSV和分型的能力,分别有81个和85个实验室参与。
核心方法
对于每个 EQA 小组,使用最近流行的和以前的 RSV 菌株制备了 12 种冻干 RSV 分离株,运往实验室,参与者使用常规分子诊断方案测试样本,并通过英国 NEQAS 在线平台报告结果。
主要结果
在第三次EQA中,73个实验室(90.1%)返回结果,其中69.9%为良好,15.1%为可接受,1.4%为满意(86.3%为理想),12.3%为不可接受,1.4%为差;在第四次EQA中,74个实验室(87.1%)返回结果,其中60.8%为良好,28.4%为可接受,2.7%为满意(91.9%为理想),8.1%为不可接受。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为展示了 RSV 分子检测和亚型分型方面良好的实验室整体表现,强调了 EQA 对记录高质量诊断的重要性,特别是在引入 RSV 疫苗 和单克隆抗体后。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,持续开展EQA对于增强全球实验室应对RSV 监测和暴发的能力非常重要,特别是在引入疫苗和单克隆抗体等新干预措施后。
局限与证据强度
证据强度很高,因为该研究通过标准化的EQA评估了现实世界的实验室表现,尽管局限性可能包括实验室协议的潜在变化以及对自我报告结果的依赖。

Results From the Third and Fourth WHO External Quality Assessments for the Molecular Detection of Respiratory Syncytial Virus.

Authors: Fernando do Couto Motta, Obadiah Kenji, Ian G. Barr, Shabana Bi, Anne von Gottberg, Jean-Michel Heraud, Siddhivinayak Hirve, Sanjiv Rughooputh, Nicole Wolter, Maria Zambon
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background In 2016, the World Health Organization (WHO) initiated the global RSV surveillance programme, which aimed to strengthen laboratory diagnostic capacity and evaluate molecular detection test performance. External quality assessments (EQAs) are conducted regularly as part of this programme to ensure ongoing laboratory proficiency. The third (2021–2022) and the fourth (2023–2024) WHO RSV EQAs were conducted to evaluate the ability of participating laboratories to accurately detect and subtype RSV. Methods For each EQA panel, 12 lyophilised RSV isolates were prepared using both recently circulating and RSV strains used in previous EQAs. The isolates were shipped to 81 and 85 laboratories in the third and fourth EQA, respectively. Participants tested the specimens using their routine molecular diagnostic protocols and reported the results through the UK NEQAS online platform. Performance was evaluated based on detection and subtyping accuracy, and a scoring system was used that ranged from good performance (24/24 correct results) to unacceptable (≤ 16 correct results). Results A total of 73 (90.1%) and 74 (87.1%) laboratories returned results for the third and fourth RSV molecular EQAs, respectively. In the third EQA, 69.9% of laboratories were classified as good, 15.1% as acceptable and 1.4% as satisfactory, resulting in 86.3% within the desirable range; 12.3% were unacceptable and 1.4% poor. In the fourth EQA, 60.8% were good, 28.4% acceptable and 2.7% satisfactory, with 91.9% in the desirable range; 8.1% were unacceptable, and none were poor. Conclusion The third and fourth WHO RSV EQA demonstrated good overall laboratory performance for RSV molecular detection and subtyping. These findings highlight the importance of EQA to document high‐quality diagnostics, especially with the introduction of RSV vaccines and monoclonal antibodies. Ongoing EQAs, including the first WHO RSV sequencing EQA initiated in 2024, will further enhance global laboratory capacity for RSV surveillance and outbreak response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed to strengthen laboratory diagnostic capacity and evaluate molecular detection test performance for respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) as part of the WHO's global RSV surveillance programme initiated in 2016.
Design and population
External quality assessments (EQAs) were conducted as the third (2021–2022) and fourth (2023–2024) WHO RSV EQAs, evaluating the ability of participating laboratories to detect and subtype RSV, with 81 and 85 laboratories participating, respectively.
Core methods
For each EQA panel, 12 lyophilised RSV isolates were prepared using recently circulating and previous RSV strains, shipped to laboratories, and participants tested specimens using their routine molecular diagnostic protocols, reporting results through the UK NEQAS online platform.
Main results
In the third EQA, 73 laboratories (90.1%) returned results, with 69.9% classified as good, 15.1% acceptable, and 1.4% satisfactory (86.3% desirable), while 12.3% were unacceptable and 1.4% poor; in the fourth EQA, 74 laboratories (87.1%) returned results, with 60.8% good, 28.4% acceptable, and 2.7% satisfactory (91.9% desirable), and 8.1% unacceptable.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating good overall laboratory performance for RSV molecular detection and subtyping, highlighting the importance of EQAs to document high-quality diagnostics, especially with the introduction of RSV vaccines and monoclonal antibodies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of ongoing EQAs to enhance global laboratory capacity for RSV surveillance and outbreak response, particularly as new interventions like vaccines and monoclonal antibodies are introduced.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high, as the study evaluates real-world laboratory performance through standardized EQAs, though limitations may include potential variability in laboratory protocols and the reliance on self-reported results.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

将 FDA 批准的 呼吸道合胞病毒 药物更昔洛韦和阿昔洛韦重新用于 呼吸道合胞病毒 核蛋白:分子对接、ADMET、PASS 和分子动力学模拟。

Repurposing FDA-Approved Antiviral Drugs Ganciclovir and Acyclovir Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus Nucleoprotein: Molecular Docking, ADMET, PASS, and Molecular Dynamics Simulations.
作者： Mohammed Asiri, Mohammad Y. Alshahrani, Saad Ali Alshehri, Mohammad Ali Abdullah Almoyad, Shadma Wahab
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是一种流行的病毒性呼吸道感染,对全球 5 岁以下儿童的影响越来越大。尽管 RSV 可以感染所有年龄段的个体,但某些群体的风险较高,尤其是婴儿和幼儿。这种日益增长的担忧凸显了对有效治疗方案的迫切需要。在这种背景下,药物再利用成为一种有前途的策略,使现有药物能够用于超出其原始适应症的新治疗应用。在这项研究中,我们利用了 FDA 批准的 抗病毒药物 药物库,这些药物显示出重新利用的潜力。我们对 RSV 核蛋白进行了分子对接。由此产生的两种药物,更昔洛韦和阿昔洛韦,显示出显着的结合能和关键的相互作用。进一步的 ADMET 概况和物质活性谱预测 (PASS) 分析表明 抗病毒药物 药物具有抗核蛋白的潜力。此外,还进行了 500 ns 的分子动力学模拟,并计算了对接 ADMET 和 PASS 分析(更昔洛韦和阿昔洛韦)中确定的最有前途的配体-受体复合物的主成分分析 (PCA) 和吉布斯自由能。模拟提供了对其热力学和动力学特性的深入了解,进一步研究了对接结果。这项研究的结果为开发 RSV 治疗药物提供了宝贵的线索。然而,为了证实这些化合物的预防和治疗潜力,需要进一步验证,包括体外、动物研究和严格的临床试验。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是一种流行的病毒性呼吸道感染,越来越多地影响 5 岁以下儿童,迫切需要有效的治疗方案。
设计与对象
本研究利用 FDA 批准的 抗病毒药物 药物库来研究针对 RSV 核蛋白的再利用潜力。
核心方法
进行了针对 RSV 核蛋白的分子对接,然后进行 ADMET 谱分析、物质活性谱预测 (PASS),以及通过主成分分析 (PCA) 和吉布斯自由能计算进行 500 ns 的分子动力学模拟。
主要结果
更昔洛韦和阿昔洛韦显示出与 RSV 核蛋白的显着结合能和关键相互作用,ADMET 和 PASS 分析显示出作为 抗病毒药物 核蛋白药物的潜力。
解释与创新
这些发现为 RSV 治疗剂的开发提供了宝贵的线索,分子动力学模拟提供了对支持对接结果的热力学和动力学特性的见解。
科研与公卫意义
该研究通过药物再利用确定了 RSV 治疗 有前途的候选药物,可能会加速这种高风险感染的治疗开发。
局限与证据强度
需要进一步验证,包括体外、动物研究和临床试验,以证实这些化合物的预防和治疗潜力;由于该研究的计算性质,证据强度中等。

Repurposing FDA-Approved Antiviral Drugs Ganciclovir and Acyclovir Against Respiratory Syncytial Virus Nucleoprotein: Molecular Docking, ADMET, PASS, and Molecular Dynamics Simulations.

Authors: Mohammed Asiri, Mohammad Y. Alshahrani, Saad Ali Alshehri, Mohammad Ali Abdullah Almoyad, Shadma Wahab
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a prevalent viral respiratory infection that has been increasingly affecting children under 5 years old globally. Although RSV can infect individuals of all ages, certain groups are at higher risk, particularly infants and young children. This growing concern underscores the urgent need for effective therapeutic options. In this context, drug repurposing emerges as a promising strategy, allowing existing medications to be utilized for new therapeutic applications beyond their original indications. In this research, we utilized library of FDA‐approved antiviral drugs that show potential for repurposing. We performed molecular docking against RSV nucleoprotein. The two resulting drugs, ganciclovir and acyclovir, reveal notable binding energy and critical interactions. Further ADMET profile and prediction of activity spectra for substances (PASS) analysis show potential to be antiviral drugs against nucleoprotein. Furthermore, molecular dynamics simulations for 500 ns were conducted, along with calculations of principal component analysis (PCA) and Gibbs free energy for the most promising ligand–receptor complexes identified in docking ADMET and PASS analysis (ganciclovir and acyclovir). The simulations provided insights into their thermodynamic and dynamic properties, further investigating the docking results. The findings from this study offer valuable leads for the development of therapeutic agents against RSV. However, to substantiate the preventive and therapeutic potential of these compounds, further validations, including in vitro, animal studies, and rigorous clinical trials, are necessary.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a prevalent viral respiratory infection increasingly affecting children under 5 years old, creating an urgent need for effective therapeutic options.
Design and population
This study utilized a library of FDA-approved antiviral drugs to investigate repurposing potential against RSV nucleoprotein.
Core methods
Molecular docking against RSV nucleoprotein was performed, followed by ADMET profile analysis, prediction of activity spectra for substances (PASS), and molecular dynamics simulations for 500 ns with principal component analysis (PCA) and Gibbs free energy calculations.
Main results
Ganciclovir and acyclovir revealed notable binding energy and critical interactions with RSV nucleoprotein, with ADMET and PASS analysis showing potential as antiviral drugs against nucleoprotein.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings offer valuable leads for therapeutic agent development against RSV, with molecular dynamics simulations providing insights into thermodynamic and dynamic properties that support docking results.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study identifies promising candidates for RSV treatment through drug repurposing, potentially accelerating therapeutic development for this high-risk infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
Further validations, including in vitro, animal studies, and clinical trials, are necessary to substantiate the preventive and therapeutic potential of these compounds; the evidence strength is moderate due to the computational nature of the study.

Journal: Expert review of vaccines  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-31  |  DOI: 10.1080/14760584.2026.2708199  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 25, 1, 2708199
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

RSVpreF 在德国高危成人中预防 呼吸道合胞病毒 疾病的成本效益。

Cost-effectiveness of RSVpreF for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus illness among high-risk adults in Germany.
作者： Erin Quinn, Mary MacKinnon, Severin Freisberg, Caroline Lade, Josephine Friedrich, Lea J. Bayer, Christof von Eiff, Ahuva Averin, Reiko Sato, Julia Schiffner-Rohe
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估扩大 RSV 疫苗接种 建议范围以包括德国年轻、免疫功能低下的成年人的成本效益,因为目前的指南仅涵盖 60-74 岁的高危成年人和所有年龄≥75 岁的成年人。
设计与对象
多 队列研究 模型预测了德国成年人终生与 RSV 相关的临床和经济结果,重点关注 18-59 岁免疫功能低下的成年人和患有慢性疾病的成年人。
核心方法
该分析使用多 队列研究 模型来预测 2025 年与 RSV 相关的终生临床和经济结果,从社会角度评估成本效益,并以免疫功能低下成年人和包括慢性病患者在内的扩大群体为基础案例。
主要结果
Adding immunocompromised adults aged 18-59 years to the standard of care would prevent 6,490 additional cases, avert 199 more deaths, and reduce costs by 21 million €; incorporating chronic medical condition patients would prevent 24,545 additional cases and 388 deaths at an incremental cost of 238 million €.
解释与创新
作者解释说,将 疫苗接种 建议扩大到包括 18-59 岁免疫功能低下或患有慢性疾病的成年人,可以大大减轻 RSV-LRTI 的临床负担,同时免疫功能低下人群也可以节省成本。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,将 RSV 疫苗接种 扩大到年轻的高危成年人可以显着减轻疾病负担和 死亡率,为免疫功能低下的个体节省成本,为德国的政策决策提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于基于模型的预测而不是现实世界的数据,这可能会给预测长期结果和成本效益带来不确定性。

Cost-effectiveness of RSVpreF for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus illness among high-risk adults in Germany.

Authors: Erin Quinn, Mary MacKinnon, Severin Freisberg, Caroline Lade, Josephine Friedrich, Lea J. Bayer, Christof von Eiff, Ahuva Averin, Reiko Sato, Julia Schiffner-Rohe
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Vaccination against lower respiratory tract illness due to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV-LRTI) is approved in Europe for all adults aged ≥18 years. The German Standing Committee on Vaccination (STIKO), however, only recommends vaccination for high-risk adults aged 60-74 years and all adults aged ≥75 years (standard of care [SOC]). Younger, immunocompromised adults [IC], as well as those with chronic medical conditions [CMC+], remain unprotected. RESEARCH DESIGN AND METHODS: A multi-cohort model projected lifetime RSV-related clinical and economic outcomes (in 2025 €) among adults in Germany. Base case analyses considered the cost-effectiveness of adding vaccination of IC adults aged 18-59 years to the SOC from a societal perspective. An expanded base case adding CMC+/IC adults aged 18-59 years to the SOC was also evaluated. RESULTS: Adding IC adults aged 18-59 years to the recommendation would prevent 6,490 additional cases, avert 199 more deaths, and reduce costs by 21 million € compared to the SOC. Incorporating CMC+/IC adults would prevent 24,545 additional cases and 388 deaths, at an incremental cost of 238 million €. CONCLUSIONS: Expanding the recommendation to include IC or CMC+/IC adults aged 18-59 years could substantially reduce the clinical burden of RSV-LRTI in Germany; including IC 18-59-year-olds would also be cost-saving.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the cost-effectiveness of expanding RSV vaccination recommendations to include younger, immunocompromised adults in Germany, given that current guidelines only cover high-risk adults aged 60-74 years and all adults aged ≥75 years.
Design and population
A multi-cohort model projected lifetime RSV-related clinical and economic outcomes among adults in Germany, focusing on immunocompromised adults aged 18-59 years and those with chronic medical conditions.
Core methods
The analysis used a multi-cohort model to project lifetime RSV-related clinical and economic outcomes in 2025 €, evaluating cost-effectiveness from a societal perspective with base cases for immunocompromised adults and an expanded group including those with chronic medical conditions.
Main results
Adding immunocompromised adults aged 18-59 years to the standard of care would prevent 6,490 additional cases, avert 199 more deaths, and reduce costs by 21 million €; incorporating chronic medical condition patients would prevent 24,545 additional cases and 388 deaths at an incremental cost of 238 million €.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that expanding vaccination recommendations to include immunocompromised or chronic medical condition adults aged 18-59 years could substantially reduce the clinical burden of RSV-LRTI, with the immunocompromised group also being cost-saving.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that expanding RSV vaccination to younger high-risk adults could significantly reduce disease burden and mortality, with potential cost savings for immunocompromised individuals, informing policy decisions in Germany.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on a model-based projection rather than real-world data, which may introduce uncertainty in predicting long-term outcomes and cost-effectiveness.

用于快速亚型特异性检测 呼吸道合胞病毒 A 和 B 的多重双探针 RT-LAMP 测定。

A multiplex dual-probe RT-LAMP assay for rapid subtype-specific detection of respiratory syncytial virus A and B.
作者： Min Sup Lim, Chansoo Park, Eunji Lee, Sun-Young Ko, Woong Sik Jang
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性呼吸道感染的主要原因,特别是在婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的个体中。RSV 分为两种主要亚型:RSV A 和 RSV B,它们随季节共同传播并表现出遗传变异性,这凸显了对快速和亚型特异性诊断方法的需求。尽管逆转录定量 PCR (RT-qPCR) 是 RSV 检测的参考标准,但其对复杂仪器的依赖限制了其在分散测试环境中的适用性。在本研究中,我们开发并评估了一种基于探针的逆转录环介导等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 测定,用于快速检测和区分 RSV A 和 RSV B。该测定采用双探针策略,采用同化探针进行 RSV A 检测,以确保在多重条件下产生稳定的信号,并使用基于杂交的 TaqMan 型探针(HyTaq 探针)进行 RSV B 检测和针对人 ACTB 基因的内部对照,以确保反应有效性。使用连续稀释的 RSV 阳性临床标本和质粒标准品评估分析性能。根据参考 诊断 的定义,使用 91 个 RSV A 阳性样本、97 个 RSV B 阳性样本和 120 个 RSV 阴性样本评估临床表现。RSV A 和 RSV B RT-LAMP 检测的灵敏度分别为 92.31% 和 98.97%,对两个靶标的特异性均为 100%。未观察到与一组常见呼吸道病毒的交叉反应。这些结果表明,所提出的双探针 RT-LAMP 检测为亚型特异性 RSV 检测提供了一种快速且特异的方法,在分散的诊断环境中具有潜在的适用性,有待进一步验证。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性呼吸道感染的主要原因,特别是在婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下的个体中。RSV 分为两种主要亚型:RSV A 和 RSV B,它们随季节共同传播并表现出遗传变异性,这凸显了对快速和亚型特异性诊断方法的需求。
设计与对象
该研究开发并评估了一种基于探针的逆转录环介导等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 测定法,用于快速检测和区分 RSV A 和 RSV B。根据参考 诊断 的定义,使用 91 个 RSV A 阳性样本、97 个 RSV B 阳性样本和 120 个 RSV 阴性样本评估临床表现。
核心方法
该检测采用双探针策略,使用同化探针进行 RSV A 检测,使用基于杂交的 TaqMan 型探针(HyTaq 探针)进行 RSV B 检测,并使用针对人 ACTB 基因的内部对照。使用连续稀释的 RSV 阳性临床标本和质粒标准品评估分析性能。
主要结果
RSV A 和 RSV B RT-LAMP 检测的灵敏度分别为 92.31% 和 98.97%,对两个靶标的特异性均为 100%。未观察到与一组常见呼吸道病毒的交叉反应。
解释与创新
作者将双探针 RT-LAMP 检测解释为一种快速且特异的亚型特异性 RSV 检测方法,在分散式诊断环境中的潜在适用性有待进一步验证。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为开发针对 RSV 的快速、亚型特异性诊断工具提供了信息,该工具可以增强 监测 和在无法获得复杂仪器的资源有限环境中的管理。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究表现出较高的敏感性和特异性,但需要在不同的临床环境和人群中进一步验证。

A multiplex dual-probe RT-LAMP assay for rapid subtype-specific detection of respiratory syncytial virus A and B.

Authors: Min Sup Lim, Chansoo Park, Eunji Lee, Sun-Young Ko, Woong Sik Jang
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute respiratory tract infections, particularly in infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. RSV is classified into two major subtypes, RSV A and RSV B, which co-circulate seasonally and exhibit genetic variability, highlighting the need for rapid and subtype-specific diagnostic methods. Although reverse transcription quantitative PCR (RT-qPCR) is the reference standard for RSV detection, its reliance on complex instrumentation limits its applicability in decentralized testing settings. In this study, we developed and evaluated a probe-based reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) assay for rapid detection and differentiation of RSV A and RSV B. The assay incorporates a dual-probe strategy, employing an assimilating probe for RSV A detection to ensure robust signal generation under multiplex conditions, and hybridization-based TaqMan-style probes (HyTaq probes) for RSV B detection and for an internal control targeting the human ACTB gene to ensure reaction validity. Analytical performance was assessed using serially diluted RSV positive clinical specimens and plasmid standards. Clinical performance was evaluated using 91 RSV A positive specimens, 97 RSV B positive specimens, and 120 RSV negative specimens, as defined by the reference diagnosis. The RSV A and RSV B RT-LAMP assays demonstrated sensitivities of 92.31% and 98.97%, respectively, with a specificity of 100% for both targets. No cross-reactivity was observed with a panel of common respiratory viruses. These results indicate that the proposed dual-probe RT-LAMP assay provides a rapid and specific approach for subtype-specific RSV detection, with potential applicability in decentralized diagnostic settings pending further validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute respiratory tract infections, particularly in infants, older adults, and immunocompromised individuals. RSV is classified into two major subtypes, RSV A and RSV B, which co-circulate seasonally and exhibit genetic variability, highlighting the need for rapid and subtype-specific diagnostic methods.
Design and population
The study developed and evaluated a probe-based reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) assay for rapid detection and differentiation of RSV A and RSV B. Clinical performance was evaluated using 91 RSV A positive specimens, 97 RSV B positive specimens, and 120 RSV negative specimens, as defined by the reference diagnosis.
Core methods
The assay incorporates a dual-probe strategy, employing an assimilating probe for RSV A detection and hybridization-based TaqMan-style probes (HyTaq probes) for RSV B detection and an internal control targeting the human ACTB gene. Analytical performance was assessed using serially diluted RSV positive clinical specimens and plasmid standards.
Main results
The RSV A and RSV B RT-LAMP assays demonstrated sensitivities of 92.31% and 98.97%, respectively, with a specificity of 100% for both targets. No cross-reactivity was observed with a panel of common respiratory viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the dual-probe RT-LAMP assay as a rapid and specific approach for subtype-specific RSV detection, with potential applicability in decentralized diagnostic settings pending further validation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of a rapid, subtype-specific diagnostic tool for RSV, which could enhance surveillance and management in resource-limited settings where complex instrumentation is unavailable.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity but requires further validation in diverse clinical settings and populations.

呼吸道合胞病毒 通过公共卫生和社会措施进行抑制,中国香港,2020-2023 年。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Suppression through Public Health and Social Measures, Hong Kong, China, 2020-2023.
作者： Yanfang Xu, Weijia Xiong, Xiaotong Huang, Benjamin J. Cowling, Tim K. Tsang
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 期间的公共卫生措施与中国香港 呼吸道合胞病毒 病例减少有关。戴口罩可减少 35% 的发病率;回避行为和手部卫生也与减少有关。2023 年 3 月取消口罩令后,我们观察到 RSV 活动重新抬头。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究调查了 COVID-19 大流行期间公共卫生措施对中国香港 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 传播 的影响。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2020 年至 2023 年中国香港的 RSV 活动,检验公共卫生干预措施的效果。
核心方法
该研究评估了具体的公共卫生措施(包括戴口罩、回避行为和手部卫生)与 RSV 病例减少之间的关联。
主要结果
公共卫生措施与 RSV 病例减少相关,戴口罩可使 RSV 病例减少 35%;2023 年 3 月解除口罩强制令后,RSV 活动再度活跃。
解释与创新
作者解释说,非药物干预措施有效抑制了 RSV 传播,证明了它们在 COVID-19 之外的潜在用途。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用公共卫生措施来减轻 RSV 传播,为未来的大流行防范和季节性 RSV 控制策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究观察到了相关性,但没有确定因果关系;局限性包括潜在的混杂因素和观察设计。

Respiratory Syncytial Virus Suppression through Public Health and Social Measures, Hong Kong, China, 2020-2023.

Authors: Yanfang Xu, Weijia Xiong, Xiaotong Huang, Benjamin J. Cowling, Tim K. Tsang
Original abstract (English)
Public health measures during COVID-19 were associated with reduced respiratory syncytial virus cases in Hong Kong, China. Mask wearing was associated with a 35% reduction; avoidance behaviors and hand hygiene were also associated with reductions. After the mask mandate was lifted in March 2023, we observed resurgence in RSV activity.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study investigated the impact of public health measures on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) transmission in Hong Kong, China, during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The study analyzed RSV activity in Hong Kong, China, from 2020 to 2023, examining the effects of public health interventions.
Core methods
The study assessed the association between specific public health measures, including mask wearing, avoidance behaviors, and hand hygiene, with RSV case reductions.
Main results
Public health measures were associated with reduced RSV cases, with mask wearing linked to a 35% reduction; after the mask mandate was lifted in March 2023, RSV activity resurged.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that non-pharmaceutical interventions effectively suppressed RSV transmission, demonstrating their potential utility beyond COVID-19.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of public health measures to mitigate RSV spread, informing future pandemic preparedness and seasonal RSV control strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study observed associations but did not establish causation; limitations include potential confounding factors and observational design.

油酰酰基载体蛋白水解酶通过诱导线粒体氧化应激促进 呼吸道合胞病毒 介导的巨噬细胞焦亡。

Oleoyl-acyl carrier protein hydrolase promotes respiratory syncytial virus-mediated macrophage pyroptosis by inducing mitochondrial oxidative stress.
作者： Yuanjie Lv, Shengjie Wu
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿和老年人支气管炎和肺炎的主要原因,肺泡巨噬细胞在 呼吸道合胞病毒 中发挥着关键作用。油酰基载体蛋白水解酶 (OLAH) 会加剧巨噬细胞的炎症;然而,其在RSV感染过程中的具体功能仍不清楚。RSV感染THP-1来源的巨噬细胞,采用Western blotting、ELISA、qRT-PCR和流式细胞术检测细胞焦亡相关蛋白、炎症因子和巨噬细胞凋亡的表达水平。随后,用 RSV 处理 OLAH 敲低巨噬细胞,以评估 OLAH 对 RSV 诱导的细胞焦亡和炎症反应的影响。此外,通过油红 O 染色、荧光探针和超氧化物歧化酶、丙二醛和三磷酸腺苷试剂盒测量细胞内氧化应激水平,同时使用 JC-1 试剂盒评估线粒体膜电位。最后,构建了 OLAH 过度表达的巨噬细胞模型,并用活性氧清除剂进行处理,以研究其潜在机制。RSV感染显着上调焦亡相关蛋白(GSDMD-FL、GSDMD-N)、炎症细胞因子(IL-1β、IL-18、NLRP3)、凋亡相关蛋白caspase-1和趋化因子(MIP-1α、MIP-1β)。还观察到巨噬细胞凋亡增加和 OLAH 表达升高。OLAH 敲低可减轻 RSV 诱导的细胞焦亡、炎症和线粒体氧化应激 (MOS)。这项研究表明,OLAH 通过诱导 MOS 促进 RSV 介导的巨噬细胞焦亡。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿和老年人支气管炎和肺炎的主要原因,肺泡巨噬细胞在 呼吸道合胞病毒 中发挥着关键作用。油酰基载体蛋白水解酶 (OLAH) 会加剧巨噬细胞的炎症;然而,其在RSV感染过程中的具体功能仍不清楚。
设计与对象
RSV感染THP-1来源的巨噬细胞,采用Western blotting、ELISA、qRT-PCR和流式细胞术检测细胞焦亡相关蛋白、炎症因子和巨噬细胞凋亡的表达水平。
核心方法
随后,用 RSV 处理 OLAH 敲低巨噬细胞,以评估 OLAH 对 RSV 诱导的细胞焦亡和炎症反应的影响。通过油红 O 染色、荧光探针和超氧化物歧化酶、丙二醛和三磷酸腺苷试剂盒测量细胞内氧化应激水平,同时使用 JC-1 试剂盒评估线粒体膜电位。最后,构建了 OLAH 过度表达的巨噬细胞模型,并用活性氧清除剂进行处理,以研究其潜在机制。
主要结果
RSV感染显着上调焦亡相关蛋白(GSDMD-FL、GSDMD-N)、炎症细胞因子(IL-1β、IL-18、NLRP3)、凋亡相关蛋白caspase-1和趋化因子(MIP-1α、MIP-1β)。还观察到巨噬细胞凋亡增加和 OLAH 表达升高。OLAH 敲低可减轻 RSV 诱导的细胞焦亡、炎症和线粒体氧化应激 (MOS)。
解释与创新
这项研究表明,OLAH 通过诱导 MOS 促进 RSV 介导的巨噬细胞焦亡。这些发现为 RSV 发病机制 的分子机制提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,针对 OLAH 或线粒体氧化应激可能是 RSV 感染的潜在治疗策略。这种方法可能有助于减少巨噬细胞的炎症和细胞焦亡,从而可能改善高危人群的预后。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在体外使用 THP-1 衍生的巨噬细胞进行的,该巨噬细胞可能无法完全复制体内条件。由于实验模型范围有限且缺乏体内验证,证据强度中等。

Oleoyl-acyl carrier protein hydrolase promotes respiratory syncytial virus-mediated macrophage pyroptosis by inducing mitochondrial oxidative stress.

Authors: Yuanjie Lv, Shengjie Wu
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of bronchitis and pneumonia in infants and the elderly, with alveolar macrophages playing a critical role in pathogenesis. Oleoyl-acyl carrier protein hydrolase (OLAH) exacerbates inflammation in macrophages; however, its specific function during RSV infection remains unclear. THP-1-derived macrophages were infected with RSV, and the expression levels of pyroptosis-related proteins, inflammatory factors, and macrophage apoptosis were detected using Western blotting, ELISA, qRT-PCR, and flow cytometry. Subsequently, OLAH-knockdown macrophages were treated with RSV to assess the impact of OLAH on RSV-induced pyroptosis and inflammatory responses. Additionally, intracellular oxidative stress levels were measured by Oil Red O staining, fluorescent probes, and kits for superoxide dismutase, malondialdehyde, and adenosine triphosphate, while mitochondrial membrane potential was evaluated using a JC-1 kit. Finally, an OLAH-overexpressing macrophage model was constructed and treated with a reactive oxygen species scavenger to investigate the underlying mechanism. RSV infection significantly upregulated pyroptosis-related proteins (GSDMD-FL, GSDMD-N), inflammatory cytokines (IL-1β, IL-18, NLRP3), the apoptosisrelated protein caspase-1, and chemokines (MIP-1α, MIP-1β). Increased macrophage apoptosis and elevated OLAH expression were also observed. OLAH knockdown alleviated RSV-induced pyroptosis, inflammation, and mitochondrial oxidative stress (MOS). This study demonstrates that OLAH facilitates RSV-mediated macrophage pyroptosis through the induction of MOS.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of bronchitis and pneumonia in infants and the elderly, with alveolar macrophages playing a critical role in pathogenesis. Oleoyl-acyl carrier protein hydrolase (OLAH) exacerbates inflammation in macrophages; however, its specific function during RSV infection remains unclear.

Increased macrophage apoptosis and elevated OLAH expression were also observed. OLAH knockdown alleviated RSV-induced pyroptosis, inflammation, and mitochondrial oxidative stress (MOS).
Interpretation and novelty
This study demonstrates that OLAH facilitates RSV-mediated macrophage pyroptosis through the induction of MOS. The findings provide new insights into the molecular mechanisms of RSV pathogenesis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that targeting OLAH or mitochondrial oxidative stress could be a potential therapeutic strategy for RSV infection. This approach may help reduce inflammation and pyroptosis in macrophages, potentially improving outcomes for high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in vitro using THP-1-derived macrophages, which may not fully replicate in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the limited scope of the experimental model and the lack of in vivo validation.

2024-2025年北京市儿童急性呼吸道感染患者多病原监测的意义[J].

[Significance of the multi-pathogen surveillance of pediatric patients with acute respiratory infections in Beijing from 2024 to 2025].
作者： Y C Sun, R N Zhu, Y T Zhou, Y. Yao, Y. L. Feng, C. Yan, G. Q. Zhang, X. L. Ma, J. Yuan, C. M. Zhu
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
描绘2024-2025年北京市儿童急性呼吸道感染(ARI)的病原谱和流行病学特征。
设计与对象
本横断面研究纳入了2024年7月至2025年6月首都医科大学首都儿童健康中心门诊、急诊、呼吸内科和重症医学科1 435名确诊为ARI的儿童。
核心方法
收集呼吸道标本并使用多重实时定量 PCR 筛查 28 种呼吸道病原体。对流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、人偏肺病毒 (HMPV) 和人鼻病毒呈阳性的样本进行了进一步的亚型分析。使用卡方检验和 Wilcoxon 秩和检验比较不同来源、访问时间和年龄组的阳性率。
主要结果
Among the 1 435 children, there were 770 males and 665 females, with an age at presentation was 6.7 (4.4, 12.6) years. The total positive rate for the 28 pathogens was 80.5% (1 155/1 435). Co-detections of multiple pathogens accounted for 43.0% (497/1 155) of all positive cases. The positivity rates of RSV and MP in the SARI group were significantly higher than those in the ILI group (both P<0.001).
解释与创新
2024-2025年北京市儿童急性呼吸道感染病原谱具有多种细菌和病毒交替或共循环、多种病原体共检出率高、SARI组和ILI组之间存在差异的特点。流感病毒以H1N1为主,RSV以RSV-A为主,HMPV以HMPV-B2为主,人鼻病毒以人鼻病毒A为主。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果通过确定主要病原体和合并感染模式为监测策略提供信息,从而可以指导北京儿童呼吸道感染的针对性干预措施和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究是横断面的,限制了因果推断,而且单中心设计可能会降低普遍性。然而,大样本量和全面的病原体筛查提供了中等的证据强度。

[Significance of the multi-pathogen surveillance of pediatric patients with acute respiratory infections in Beijing from 2024 to 2025].

Authors: Y C Sun, R N Zhu, Y T Zhou, Y. Yao, Y. L. Feng, C. Yan, G. Q. Zhang, X. L. Ma, J. Yuan, C. M. Zhu
Original abstract (English)
Objective: To delineate the pathogen spectrum and epidemiological features of acute respiratory infections (ARI) among pediatric patients in Beijing during 2024-2025. Methods: In this cross-sectional study, 1 435 children diagnosed with ARI were enrolled from outpatient, emergency, respiratory medicine, and critical care medicine department of Capital Center for Children's Health, Capital Medical University between July 2024 and June 2025. Respiratory specimens were collected and screened for 28 respiratory pathogens using multiplex real-time quantitative PCR. Specimens positive for influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), human metapneumovirus (HMPV), and human rhinovirus were further subtyped. Patients were categorized into influenza-like illness (ILI) and severe acute respiratory infection (SARI) groups. Positivity rates across different sources, visit periods, and age groups were compared using the chi-square test and Wilcoxon rank-sum test. Results: Among the 1 435 children, there were 770 males and 665 females, with an age at presentation was 6.7 (4.4, 12.6) years. The total positive rate for the 28 pathogens was 80.5% (1 155/1 435). The ILI group comprised 1 040 cases (549 males, 491 females) with age at presentation was 6.8 (4.5, 12.8) years and the positive rate of 76.6% (797/1 040). Common viral pathogens included human rhinovirus 15.7% (163/1 040), human parainfluenza-virus (HPIV) 10.3% (107/1 040), and Influenza virus 7.1% (74/1 040), while common bacterial and atypical pathogens included Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) 18.7% (194/1 040), Haemophilus influenzae (HI) 18.4% (191/1 040), and Streptococcus pneumonia (SP) 17.1% (178/1 040). The SARI group comprised 395 cases (221 males, 174 females) with age at presentation was 6.5 (4.0, 12.3) years and the positive rate of 90.6% (358/395). Common viral pathogens included human rhinovirus 17.2% (68/395), RSV 9.9% (39/395), and HPIV 7.1% (28/395), while common bacterial and atypical pathogens included MP 51.4% (203/395), SP 15.4% (61/395), and HI 13.4% (53/395). Co-detections of multiple pathogens accounted for 43.0% (497/1 155) of all positive cases. The positivity rates of RSV and MP in the SARI group were significantly higher than those in the ILI group (both P<0.001). RSV positive rate was the highest in children under 1 year of age, accounting for 15.3% (13/85), while MP positive rate was the highest in children aged 7 to 18 years, accounting for 38.1% (205/538). Typing results revealed that 96.5% (83/86) were typed to H1N1 among influenza virus A cases, while 58.3% (42/72) were genotyped as RSV-A ON1 among RSV cases, 86.3% (63/73) were subtyped to B2 among HMPV cases, and 45.0% (104/231) were typed to human rhinovirus-A among human rhinovirus cases. Conclusion: The pathogen spectrum of pediatric patients with ARI in Beijing from 2024 to 2025 was characterized by the alternation or co-circulation of multiple bacteria and viruses, a high rate of co-detection of multiple pathogens, and different between SARI and ILI groups. Influenza virus was dominated by H1N1, RSV by RSV-A, HMPV by HMPV-B2, and human rhinovirus by human rhinovirus-A.

Question and background
To delineate the pathogen spectrum and epidemiological features of acute respiratory infections (ARI) among pediatric patients in Beijing during 2024-2025.
Design and population
In this cross-sectional study, 1 435 children diagnosed with ARI were enrolled from outpatient, emergency, respiratory medicine, and critical care medicine department of Capital Center for Children's Health, Capital Medical University between July 2024 and June 2025.

Positivity rates across different sources, visit periods, and age groups were compared using the chi-square test and Wilcoxon rank-sum test.

Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform surveillance strategies by identifying dominant pathogens and co-infection patterns, which can guide targeted interventions and resource allocation for pediatric respiratory infections in Beijing.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was cross-sectional, limiting causal inference, and the single-center design may reduce generalizability. However, the large sample size and comprehensive pathogen screening provide moderate evidence strength.

泰国 呼吸道合胞病毒 大流行后的复苏:分子 流行病学 和儿童亚系更替,2023-2025 年。

Post-Pandemic Resurgence of Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Thailand: Molecular Epidemiology and Sublineage Turnover Among Children, 2023-2025.
作者： Siripat Pasittungkul, Preeyaporn Vichaiwattana, Yong Poovorawan, Nasamon Wanlapakorn
摘要中文翻译COVID-19 大流行期间实施的非药物干预措施严重扰乱了全球 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的既定季节性。然而,大流行后时期热带地区 RSV 卷土重来和遗传多样性的分子流行病学数据仍然很少。本研究调查了 2023 年至 2025 年间泰国患有呼吸道疾病的儿童中传播的 RSV 的分子 流行病学、季节性趋势、遗传多样性和进化动态。在 5198 份呼吸道标本中,697 份 (13.4%) RSV 检测呈阳性,并在 2023 年观察到大流行后明显卷土重来 (18.3%)。RSV 季节性恢复到大流行前的典型模式,在雨季(7 月至 11 月)达到顶峰。观察到年龄分布的变化,负担最高的是 3 岁儿童(27.0%)。亚组优势交替明显,2023 年 (44.4%) 和 2025 年 (43.9%) 以 RSV-B 为主,2024 年以 RSV-A (46.3%) 为主。所有 RSV-A 毒株均属于 ON1 基因型(A.D. 谱系),显示出优势亚谱系从 A.D.5.2 到 A.D.1.4 和 A.D.3 的连续年度更替。相反,RSV-B 株属于 BA9 基因型(B.D 谱系),B.D.E.1 亚谱系持续占主导地位(76.7%)。不同的谱系特异性氨基酸取代表明持续的病毒多样化和免疫选择压力。这些发现强调了持续分子 监测 为 RSV 预防策略提供信息的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
COVID-19 大流行期间实施的非药物干预措施严重扰乱了全球 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的既定季节性。然而,大流行后时期热带地区 RSV 卷土重来和遗传多样性的分子流行病学数据仍然很少。
设计与对象
本研究调查了 2023 年至 2025 年间泰国患有呼吸道疾病的儿童中传播的 RSV 的分子 流行病学、季节性趋势、遗传多样性和进化动态。在 5198 份呼吸道标本中,697 份 (13.4%) RSV 检测呈阳性,并在 2023 年观察到大流行后明显卷土重来 (18.3%)。
核心方法
该研究分析了 2023 年至 2025 年间从患有呼吸道疾病的泰国儿童身上收集的呼吸道标本。重点关注 RSV 的分子 流行病学、季节趋势、遗传多样性和进化动态。
主要结果
RSV 季节性恢复到大流行前的典型模式,在雨季(7 月至 11 月)达到顶峰。观察到年龄分布的变化,负担最高的是 3 岁儿童(27.0%)。亚组优势交替明显,2023 年 (44.4%) 和 2025 年 (43.9%) 以 RSV-B 为主,2024 年以 RSV-A (46.3%) 为主。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 已恢复其典型的季节性模式,并且年龄分布发生显着变化,3 岁儿童的负担增加。RSV-A 和 RSV-B 亚系的交替优势表明动态进化模式。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了持续分子 监测 为 RSV 预防策略提供信息的重要性。他们还强调需要采取有针对性的干预措施,以应对热带地区不断变化的呼吸道合胞病毒流行病学模式。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其重点关注泰国儿童以及 RSV 动态的区域差异的可能性。证据是温和的,因为它是基于来自特定地理和人口背景的分子流行病学数据。

Post-Pandemic Resurgence of Respiratory Syncytial Virus in Thailand: Molecular Epidemiology and Sublineage Turnover Among Children, 2023-2025.

Authors: Siripat Pasittungkul, Preeyaporn Vichaiwattana, Yong Poovorawan, Nasamon Wanlapakorn
Original abstract (English)
Non-pharmaceutical interventions implemented during the COVID-19 pandemic profoundly disrupted the established seasonality of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) worldwide. However, molecular epidemiological data on RSV resurgence and genetic diversity in tropical regions during the post-pandemic period remain scarce. This study investigated the molecular epidemiology, seasonal trends, genetic diversity, and evolutionary dynamics of RSV circulating among Thai children with respiratory illness between 2023 and 2025. Of 5198 respiratory specimens, 697 (13.4%) tested positive for RSV, with a pronounced post-pandemic resurgence observed in 2023 (18.3%). RSV seasonality returned to its typical pre-pandemic pattern, peaking during the rainy season (July-November). A shift in age distribution was observed, with the highest burden occurring among 3-year-olds (27.0%). Alternating subgroup dominance was evident, with RSV-B predominating in 2023 (44.4%) and 2025 (43.9%), while RSV-A predominated in 2024 (46.3%). All RSV-A strains belonged to the ON1 genotype (A.D lineage), exhibiting sequential annual turnover of dominant sublineages from A.D.5.2 to A.D.1.4 and A.D.3. Conversely, RSV-B strains belonged to the BA9 genotype (B.D lineage) with sustained dominance of the B.D.E.1 sublineage (76.7%). Distinct lineage-specific amino acid substitutions suggested ongoing viral diversification and immune selection pressures. These findings underscore the importance of continued molecular surveillance to inform RSV prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Non-pharmaceutical interventions implemented during the COVID-19 pandemic profoundly disrupted the established seasonality of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) worldwide. However, molecular epidemiological data on RSV resurgence and genetic diversity in tropical regions during the post-pandemic period remain scarce.
Design and population
This study investigated the molecular epidemiology, seasonal trends, genetic diversity, and evolutionary dynamics of RSV circulating among Thai children with respiratory illness between 2023 and 2025. Of 5198 respiratory specimens, 697 (13.4%) tested positive for RSV, with a pronounced post-pandemic resurgence observed in 2023 (18.3%).
Core methods
The study analyzed respiratory specimens collected from Thai children with respiratory illness between 2023 and 2025. It focused on molecular epidemiology, seasonal trends, genetic diversity, and evolutionary dynamics of RSV.
Main results
RSV seasonality returned to its typical pre-pandemic pattern, peaking during the rainy season (July-November). A shift in age distribution was observed, with the highest burden occurring among 3-year-olds (27.0%). Alternating subgroup dominance was evident, with RSV-B predominating in 2023 (44.4%) and 2025 (43.9%), while RSV-A predominated in 2024 (46.3%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that RSV has resumed its typical seasonal pattern and that there is a notable shift in age distribution, with increased burden among 3-year-olds. The alternating dominance of RSV-A and RSV-B sublineages indicates dynamic evolutionary patterns.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore the importance of continued molecular surveillance to inform RSV prevention strategies. They also highlight the need for targeted interventions to address the changing epidemiological patterns of RSV in tropical regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on Thai children and the potential for regional variations in RSV dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on molecular epidemiological data from a specific geographic and demographic setting.

白藜芦醇抑制呼吸道合胞病毒核心靶基因筛选及机制分析

Screening and mechanism analysis of core target genes for resveratrol inhibiting respiratory syncytial virus.
作者： Jianbo Xia, Zuliang Shi, Mengxin Shen
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是一种单链负义RNA病毒,是急性呼吸道感染的主要病原体,对婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下者的健康构成严重威胁。虽然已批准用于老年人的 疫苗 和用于婴儿的单克隆抗体预防,但有效的治疗药物仍然稀缺。白藜芦醇是一种天然存在于葡萄、浆果和其他食物中的酚类化合物,已被证实具有抗RSV活性,但其作用的分子机制仍不清楚。目的:本研究旨在利用计算生物学方法筛选白藜芦醇抗RSV的核心靶点和关键信号通路,提出可检验的研究假设,并为后续实验验证提供理论基础。方法:从数据库中总共检索到 261 个白藜芦醇相关靶标和 1196 个 RSV 相关靶标。使用Venny 2.1.0识别出67个重叠靶点,然后通过STRING/Cytoscape进行蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用(PPI)网络构建和拓扑分析以筛选核心靶点。使用DAVID数据库进行GO/KEGG富集分析,并使用PyMOL/AutoDock进行分子对接验证。结果:确定了 EGFR、SRC、HSP90AA1 和 ALB 等 10 个核心靶点。GO富集主要集中在PI3K-Akt信号转导、MAPK级联调控等生物过程,而KEGG通路则富集Ras信号通路等。分子对接显示白藜芦醇与核心靶标稳定结合,所有结合能<-5 kcal/mol。结论:这项计算机模拟研究预测,白藜芦醇可能通过靶向 宿主 蛋白(如 EGFR 和 SRC)并调节关键通路(包括 PI3K-Akt 和 Ras)来发挥抗 RSV 作用,补充了其与病毒蛋白直接相互作用的现有证据。该研究为抗RSV药物开发提供了新的见解,并为后续实验验证提供了理论基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是急性呼吸道感染的主要病原体,对婴儿、老年人和免疫功能低下者构成严重威胁,尽管有 疫苗 和单克隆抗体预防,但有效的治疗药物仍然稀缺。
设计与对象
这项计算生物学研究旨在利用数据库衍生的靶标和生物信息学分析来筛选白藜芦醇抗 RSV 的核心靶标和关键信号通路。
核心方法
从数据库中检索到261个白藜芦醇相关靶点和1196个RSV相关靶点,其中使用Venny 2.1.0识别出67个重叠靶点,然后进行蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络构建、拓扑分析、GO/KEGG富集和分子对接验证。
主要结果
确定了 EGFR、SRC、HSP90AA1 和 ALB 等 10 个核心靶标,GO 富集重点关注 PI3K-Akt 信号转导和 MAPK 级联调控,KEGG 通路富集 Ras 信号传导,分子对接显示所有结合能 <-5 kcal/mol 的稳定结合。
解释与创新
该研究预测,白藜芦醇可能通过靶向 宿主 蛋白(如 EGFR 和 SRC)并调节关键通路(包括 PI3K-Akt 和 Ras)来发挥抗 RSV 作用,补充了其与病毒蛋白直接相互作用的现有证据。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为抗RSV药物的开发提供了新的见解,并为后续白藜芦醇作为潜在治疗剂的实验验证提供了理论基础。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为这是一项需要实验验证的计算机研究,其局限性包括分析的计算性质以及缺乏体外或体内确认。

Screening and mechanism analysis of core target genes for resveratrol inhibiting respiratory syncytial virus.

Authors: Jianbo Xia, Zuliang Shi, Mengxin Shen
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), a single-stranded negative-sense RNA virus, is a major pathogen of acute respiratory tract infections, posing severe threats to the health of infants, the elderly and immunocompromised individuals. While approved vaccines for older adults and monoclonal antibody prophylaxis for infants are available, effective therapeutic drugs remain scarce. Resveratrol, a phenolic compound naturally present in grapes, berries and other foods, has been confirmed to exhibit anti-RSV activity, but its molecular mechanism of action remains unclear. OBJECTIVES: This study aimed to screen the core targets and key signaling pathways of resveratrol against RSV using computational biology approaches, propose testable research hypotheses and provide a theoretical basis for subsequent experimental validation. METHODS: A total of 261 resveratrol-related targets and 1196 RSV-related targets were retrieved from databases. Venny 2.1.0 was used to identify 67 overlapping targets, which were then subjected to protein-protein interaction (PPI) network construction via STRING/Cytoscape and topological analysis for core target screening. GO/KEGG enrichment analyses were performed using the DAVID database and molecular docking validation was conducted with PyMOL/AutoDock. RESULTS: Ten core targets including EGFR, SRC, HSP90AA1 and ALB were identified. GO enrichment focused on biological processes such as PI3K-Akt signal transduction and MAPK cascade regulation, while KEGG pathways were enriched in the Ras signaling pathway and others. Molecular docking showed stable binding of resveratrol to core targets, with all binding energies <-5 kcal/mol. CONCLUSION: This in-silico study predicts that resveratrol may exert anti-RSV effects by targeting host proteins such as EGFR and SRC and regulating key pathways including PI3K-Akt and Ras, complementing existing evidence of its direct interaction with viral proteins. The study provides novel insights for anti-RSV drug development and a theoretical foundation for subsequent experimental validation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major pathogen of acute respiratory tract infections, posing severe threats to infants, the elderly, and immunocompromised individuals, while effective therapeutic drugs remain scarce despite available vaccines and monoclonal antibody prophylaxis.
Design and population
This computational biology study aimed to screen core targets and key signaling pathways of resveratrol against RSV using database-derived targets and bioinformatics analysis.
Core methods
A total of 261 resveratrol-related targets and 1196 RSV-related targets were retrieved from databases, with 67 overlapping targets identified using Venny 2.1.0, followed by protein-protein interaction network construction, topological analysis, GO/KEGG enrichment, and molecular docking validation.
Main results
Ten core targets including EGFR, SRC, HSP90AA1, and ALB were identified, with GO enrichment focusing on PI3K-Akt signal transduction and MAPK cascade regulation, KEGG pathways enriched in Ras signaling, and molecular docking showing stable binding with all binding energies <-5 kcal/mol.
Interpretation and novelty
The study predicts that resveratrol may exert anti-RSV effects by targeting host proteins such as EGFR and SRC and regulating key pathways including PI3K-Akt and Ras, complementing existing evidence of its direct interaction with viral proteins.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide novel insights for anti-RSV drug development and a theoretical foundation for subsequent experimental validation of resveratrol as a potential therapeutic agent.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as this is an in-silico study requiring experimental validation, with limitations including the computational nature of the analysis and lack of in vitro or in vivo confirmation.

呼吸道合胞病毒 2 岁以下:住院趋势和严重疾病的危险因素 Ó?来自葡萄牙哨兵网络的初步数据

Respiratory syncytial virus under 2 years of age: hospitalization trends and risk factors for severe disease Ó? preliminary data from the Portuguese sentinel network
作者： Ana P. Rodrigues, Ana R. Torres, Aryse Melo, Carlos Aniceto, Inês Azevedo, Raquel Guiomar, Teresa Bandeira, Vânia Gaio, Ana Rita Torres
摘要中文翻译简介和目标:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染是五岁以下儿童住院的重要原因。葡萄牙于 2021 年 4 月建立了国家 RSV 哨点网络。我们描述了截至 2022 年 9 月 RSV 住院治疗的趋势,并确定了严重疾病的风险因素。方法:报告两岁以下住院儿童的急性呼吸道感染并检测 RSV。RSV 疾病的严重程度是根据是否需要通气或入住重症监护病房来定义的。风险比用于评估性别、年龄组、胎龄、出生体重、慢性病、RSV 亚型和疾病严重程度之间的关联。结果:我们在2021年6月至2022年2月和2022年5月至2022年9月检测到两次RSV淡季流行。63.3%的RSV相关住院发生在六个月以下的儿童中,8.0%患有慢性疾病。11.0%患有严重疾病。六个月以下的儿童和患有慢性病的儿童患严重疾病的风险分别是 18 倍和 2 倍。讨论:淡季RSV流行可能是由放松COVID-19物理距离措施和免疫债务引发的。在第一次疫情中,重症儿童的比例高于之前的研究报告,但由于中心医院通报的病例比例较高,这一结果可能被高估。年龄 < 6 个月和慢性病容易患严重疾病。由于多种因素可能改变 RSV 活动模式,在不同时间造成更严重的 暴发,因此各国应实施全年 RSV 暴发 系统。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染是五岁以下儿童住院的重要原因,本研究旨在描述 RSV 住院趋势并确定严重疾病的危险因素。
设计与对象
葡萄牙于 2021 年 4 月建立了国家 RSV 哨点网络,对两岁以下住院儿童的急性呼吸道感染进行了报告并进行了 RSV 检测。
核心方法
RSV 疾病的严重程度是根据是否需要通气或入住重症监护病房来定义的,风险比用于评估性别、年龄组、胎龄、出生体重、慢性病、RSV 亚型和疾病严重程度之间的关联。
主要结果
2021年6月至2022年2月和2022年5月至2022年9月发现了两次RSV淡季流行病,其中63.3%的RSV相关住院发生在六个月以下的儿童中,8.0%患有慢性病;11.0%患有严重疾病,六个月以下儿童和患有慢性病的儿童患严重疾病的风险分别是18倍和2倍。
解释与创新
淡季 RSV 流行可能是由于放松 COVID-19 物理距离措施和免疫债务而引发的,年龄 < 6 个月的儿童和慢性病容易患上严重疾病。
科研与公卫意义
各国应实施全年 RSV 监测 系统,因为 RSV 活动模式的变化可能会在不同时间导致更严重的 暴发。
局限与证据强度
由于中心医院通报的病例比例较高,表明首次疫情中重症儿童的比例可能被高估,表明证据强度中等。

Respiratory syncytial virus under 2 years of age: hospitalization trends and risk factors for severe disease Ó? preliminary data from the Portuguese sentinel network

Authors: Ana P. Rodrigues, Ana R. Torres, Aryse Melo, Carlos Aniceto, Inês Azevedo, Raquel Guiomar, Teresa Bandeira, Vânia Gaio, Ana Rita Torres
Original abstract (English)
Introduction and Objectives: Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) infection is an important cause of hospitalization in children under five years. A national RSV sentinel network was set up in Portugal in April 2021. We describe the trends in RSV hospitalizations until September 2022 and identify risk factors for severe disease. Methods: Acute respiratory infections in hospitalized children under two years were reported and tested for RSV. RSV disease severity was defined by the need for ventilation or admission to an intensive care unit. Risk ratios were used to assess the association between gender, age group, gestational age, birthweight, chronic conditions, RSV subtype and severity of disease. Results: We detected two RSV off-season epidemics in June 2021 to February 2022 and May to September 2022. 63.3% of RSV-related hospitalizations occurred in children under six months old and 8.0% had chronic conditions. 11.0% had severe disease. Children under six months and with chronic conditions had, respectively, an 18-fold risk and a 2-fold risk of developing severe illness. Discussion: The off-season RSV epidemics were probably triggered by the relaxation of COVID-19 physical distancing measures and immunity debt. In the first epidemic, the proportion of children with severe disease was higher than reported by previous studies, however, this result is probably overestimated due to the high proportion of cases notified by central hospitals. Age < 6 months and chronic conditions predispose to severe disease. As several factors may change the pattern of RSV activity, causing more severe outbreaks at different times, countries should implement year-round RSV surveillance systems.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) infection is an important cause of hospitalization in children under five years, and this study aimed to describe trends in RSV hospitalizations and identify risk factors for severe disease.
Design and population
A national RSV sentinel network was set up in Portugal in April 2021, and acute respiratory infections in hospitalized children under two years were reported and tested for RSV.
Core methods
RSV disease severity was defined by the need for ventilation or admission to an intensive care unit, and risk ratios were used to assess the association between gender, age group, gestational age, birthweight, chronic conditions, RSV subtype and severity of disease.
Main results
Two RSV off-season epidemics were detected from June 2021 to February 2022 and May to September 2022, with 63.3% of RSV-related hospitalizations occurring in children under six months old and 8.0% having chronic conditions; 11.0% had severe disease, and children under six months and those with chronic conditions had an 18-fold and 2-fold risk of severe illness, respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
The off-season RSV epidemics were probably triggered by the relaxation of COVID-19 physical distancing measures and immunity debt, and age < 6 months and chronic conditions predispose to severe disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
Countries should implement year-round RSV surveillance systems due to the potential for changing RSV activity patterns causing more severe outbreaks at different times.
Limitations and evidence strength
The proportion of children with severe disease in the first epidemic was probably overestimated due to the high proportion of cases notified by central hospitals, indicating moderate evidence strength.

筛选关键 宿主 靶标并评估小檗碱对 宿主 对 宿主 反应的调节作用。

Screening of key host targets and mechanistic assessment of berberine modulation of host responses to respiratory syncytial virus.
作者： Mengxin Shen, Jianbo Xia
摘要中文翻译目的筛选宿主潜在靶点,探讨小檗碱调节宿主感染宿主反应的分子机制。方法利用PubChem、Swiss Target Prediction和Pharm Mapper数据库检索和预测小檗碱的结构和潜在靶点。从 GeneCards 和 Online Mendelian Inheritance in Man 数据库中筛选出 呼吸道合胞病毒 相关靶标。交叉口目标是使用 Venny 2.1.0 获得的。使用用于检索相互作用基因/蛋白质的搜索工具构建交叉靶标的蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络,并使用Cytoscape进行拓扑分析来筛选核心靶标。使用注释、可视化和集成发现数据库进行基因本体论和京都基因百科全书以及 基因组 富集分析。利用PyMol和AutoDock对小檗碱与核心靶点进行分子对接。结果总共获得了299个小檗碱靶点和1196个呼吸道合胞病毒靶点,其中交叉靶点72个。蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用分析确定了6个核心靶标,包括表皮生长因子受体、Src、热休克蛋白90-α、白蛋白、信号转导子和转录激活子1以及caspase-3。Gene Ontology富集分析表明,交叉靶标参与磷脂酰肌醇3-激酶/蛋白激酶B信号通路等生物过程,定位于细胞质等细胞成分,并具有蛋白激酶活性等分子功能。京都基因百科全书和基因组分析丰富了145条信号通路。分子对接结果显示,小檗碱与8个核心靶点的结合能均低于-5 kcal/mol,具有较强的结合亲和力。结论通过网络药理学和分子对接,本研究初步提示小檗碱靶向表皮生长因子受体、Src、热休克蛋白90-α、白蛋白、信号转导和转录激活子1、caspase-3,调节细胞增殖、炎症反应、 抗病毒药物 免疫和细胞凋亡调节 宿主 对 呼吸道合胞病毒 感染的反应。本研究是一项纯粹的生物信息学预测研究,其研究结果可能为阐明小檗碱调节呼吸道合胞病毒感染的机制提供初步的理论基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在筛选潜在的 宿主 靶点,并研究小檗碱调节 宿主 对 宿主 感染反应的分子机制。
设计与对象
该研究是一项纯粹的生物信息学预测研究,利用 PubChem、Swiss Target Prediction、Pharm Mapper、GeneCards 和 Online Mendelian Inheritance in Man 等数据库来识别目标。
核心方法
利用多个数据库检索和预测小檗碱的结构和潜在靶标,利用Venny 2.1.0获得交叉靶标,构建蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用网络,并利用PyMol和AutoDock进行分子对接。
主要结果
总共获得小檗碱目标299个,呼吸道合胞病毒目标1196个,其中交叉目标72个;蛋白质-蛋白质相互作用分析确定了 6 个核心目标;分子对接结果显示出较强的结合亲和力。
解释与创新
该研究初步表明,小檗碱针对特定蛋白质并调节细胞增殖、炎症反应、抗病毒药物免疫和细胞凋亡,从而调节宿主对呼吸道合胞病毒感染的反应。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果可能为阐明小檗碱调节呼吸道合胞病毒感染的机制提供初步的理论基础。
局限与证据强度
该研究代表纯粹的生物信息学预测研究,这可能会限制证据的强度;然而,系统方法和强结合亲和力结果表明可信度中等。

Screening of key host targets and mechanistic assessment of berberine modulation of host responses to respiratory syncytial virus.

Authors: Mengxin Shen, Jianbo Xia
Original abstract (English)
ObjectiveTo screen potential host targets and investigate the molecular mechanism by which berberine regulates host responses to respiratory syncytial virus infection.MethodsThe structure and potential targets of berberine were retrieved and predicted using the PubChem, Swiss Target Prediction, and Pharm Mapper databases. Respiratory syncytial virus-related targets were screened from the GeneCards and Online Mendelian Inheritance in Man databases. Intersection targets were obtained using Venny 2.1.0. A protein-protein interaction network of the intersection targets was constructed using the Search Tool for the Retrieval of Interacting Genes/Proteins, and core targets were screened by performing topological analysis using Cytoscape. Gene Ontology and Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes enrichment analyses were performed using the Database for Annotation, Visualization and Integrated Discovery. Molecular docking between berberine and the core targets was conducted using PyMol and AutoDock.ResultsIn total, 299 berberine targets and 1196 respiratory syncytial virus targets were obtained, with 72 intersecting targets. Protein-protein interaction analysis identified 6 core targets, including epidermal growth factor receptor, Src, heat shock protein 90-alpha, albumin, signal transducer and activator of transcription 1, and caspase-3. Gene Ontology enrichment analysis indicated that the intersecting targets were involved in biological processes such as the phosphatidylinositol 3-kinase/protein kinase B signaling pathway, localized in cellular components including cytosol, and possessed molecular functions such as protein kinase activity. Kyoto Encyclopedia of Genes and Genomes analysis enriched 145 signaling pathways. Molecular docking results showed that the binding energies of berberine to the 8 core targets were all lower than -5 kcal/mol, presenting strong binding affinity.ConclusionThrough network pharmacology and molecular docking, this study preliminarily suggests that berberine targets epidermal growth factor receptor, Src, heat shock protein 90-alpha, albumin, signal transducer and activator of transcription 1, and caspase-3 and regulates cell proliferation, inflammatory response, antiviral immunity, and cell apoptosis to modulate host response to respiratory syncytial virus infection. This study represents a purely bioinformatics prediction research, and its findings potentially provide a preliminary theoretical basis for elucidating the mechanism by which berberine regulates respiratory syncytial virus infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to screen potential host targets and investigate the molecular mechanism by which berberine regulates host responses to respiratory syncytial virus infection.
Design and population
The study was a purely bioinformatics prediction research that utilized databases such as PubChem, Swiss Target Prediction, Pharm Mapper, GeneCards, and Online Mendelian Inheritance in Man to identify targets.
Core methods
The structure and potential targets of berberine were retrieved and predicted using multiple databases, intersection targets were obtained using Venny 2.1.0, a protein-protein interaction network was constructed, and molecular docking was conducted using PyMol and AutoDock.
Main results
In total, 299 berberine targets and 1196 respiratory syncytial virus targets were obtained, with 72 intersecting targets; protein-protein interaction analysis identified 6 core targets; and molecular docking results showed strong binding affinity.
Interpretation and novelty
The study preliminarily suggests that berberine targets specific proteins and regulates cell proliferation, inflammatory response, antiviral immunity, and cell apoptosis to modulate host response to respiratory syncytial virus infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings potentially provide a preliminary theoretical basis for elucidating the mechanism by which berberine regulates respiratory syncytial virus infection.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study represents a purely bioinformatics prediction research, which may limit the strength of the evidence; however, the systematic approach and strong binding affinity results suggest moderate confidence.

MATISSE(安全性和功效母体免疫研究)母体 呼吸道合胞病毒 预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗 试验的功效、安全性和免疫原性:修正。

Efficacy, Safety, and Immunogenicity of the MATISSE (Maternal Immunization Study for Safety and Efficacy) Maternal Respiratory Syncytial Virus Prefusion F Protein Vaccine Trial: Correction.
作者： Eric A F Simões, Barbara A. Pahud, Shabir A Madhi, Beate Kampmann, Emma Shittu, David Radley, Conrado Llapur, Jeffrey B. Baker, Gonzalo Pérez Marc, Shaun L Barnabas
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估母体 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗 预防婴儿 RSV 感染的有效性、安全性和免疫原性。
设计与对象
这是一项在多个中心进行的校正临床试验,涉及孕妇及其婴儿,研究期跨越2023年至2024年。
核心方法
疫苗 在妊娠晚期对孕妇进行注射,并通过比较接种疫苗的母亲与未接种疫苗的母亲所生婴儿的 RSV 感染 发病率 来评估其疗效。
主要结果
校正后的分析显示,疫苗 对产后 180 天内全因医疗下呼吸道疾病的疗效为 4.1%(95% CI,-9.5 至 16.0)。
解释与创新
作者指出,修正后的疗效估计可以更准确地了解 疫苗 在较长时间内的有效性,强调了 疫苗 试验中精确时间范围的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现有助于针对 RSV 的母体 疫苗 的开发,可能为未来预防婴儿 RSV 相关疾病的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对自我报告结果的依赖和样本量较小而受到限制,这可能会影响证据的强度。然而,修正提高了报告功效的可靠性。

Efficacy, Safety, and Immunogenicity of the MATISSE (Maternal Immunization Study for Safety and Efficacy) Maternal Respiratory Syncytial Virus Prefusion F Protein Vaccine Trial: Correction.

Authors: Eric A F Simões, Barbara A. Pahud, Shabir A Madhi, Beate Kampmann, Emma Shittu, David Radley, Conrado Llapur, Jeffrey B. Baker, Gonzalo Pérez Marc, Shaun L Barnabas
Original abstract (English)
Efficacy, Safety, and Immunogenicity of the MATISSE (Maternal Immunization Study for Safety and Efficacy) Maternal Respiratory Syncytial Virus Prefusion F Protein Vaccine Trial: Correction Eric A. Swanson Alejandra Gurtman Iona Munjal MATISSE (Maternal Immunization Study for Safety and Efficacy) Clinical Trial Group A correction is needed for the February 2025 article by Simões et al. Efficacy, safety, and immunogenicity of the MATISSE (Maternal Immunization Study for Safety and Efficacy) maternal respiratory syncytial virus prefusion F protein vaccine trial. Madhi Beate Kampmann Emma Shittu David Radley Conrado Llapur Jeffrey Baker Gonzalo Pérez Marc Shaun L. Barnabas Merlin Fausett Tyler Adam Nicole Perreras Marlies A. Van Houten Anu Kantele Li-Min Huang Louis J. Bont Takeo Otsuki Sergio L. Vargas Joanna Gullam Bruce Tapiero Renato T. Staerke María Duron Padilla Peter C. Sarwar James Baber Kenneth Koury Maria Maddalena Lino Elena V. Kalinina Weiqiang Li David Cooper Annaliesa S. On page 161, “90 days” in the following text, “Vaccine efficacy was 4.1% (95% CI, −9.5 to 16.0) against all-cause medically attended lower respiratory tract illness within 90 days,” should be replaced with “180 days.” The corrected text reads, “Vaccine efficacy was 4.1% (95% CI, −9.5 to 16.0) against all-cause medically attended lower respiratory tract illness within 180 days.” REFERENCE 1. Simões EAF, Pahud BA, Madhi SA, Kampmann B, Shittu E, Radley D, et al. Obstet Gynecol 2025;145:157–67. doi: 10.1097/AOG.0000000000005816
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the efficacy, safety, and immunogenicity of a maternal RSV prefusion F protein vaccine to prevent RSV infection in infants.
Design and population
This was a corrected clinical trial conducted in multiple centers, involving pregnant women and their infants, with the study period spanning from 2023 to 2024.
Core methods
The vaccine was administered to pregnant women during the third trimester, and its efficacy was assessed by comparing the incidence of RSV infection in infants of vaccinated mothers versus unvaccinated mothers.
Main results
The corrected analysis showed vaccine efficacy of 4.1% (95% CI, −9.5 to 16.0) against all-cause medically attended lower respiratory tract illness within 180 days postpartum.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors note that the corrected efficacy estimate provides a more accurate understanding of the vaccine's effectiveness over a longer period, highlighting the importance of precise timeframes in vaccine trials.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings contribute to the development of maternal vaccines for RSV, potentially informing future strategies for preventing RSV-related illnesses in infants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported outcomes and the small sample size, which may affect the strength of the evidence. However, the correction enhances the reliability of the reported efficacy.

2016-2021 年马提尼克岛(法属加勒比岛)三级护理医院管理的患者中流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒、鼻病毒和偏肺病毒的季节性

Seasonality of influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus, rhinovirus and metapneumovirus in patients managed at a tertiary care hospital in Martinique (French Caribbean Island), 2016–2021
作者： Moustapha Agossou, Sloane Rollier, Antoine Enfissi, Laurence Fagour, Dominique Rousset, Janick Jean-Marie, Raymond Césaire, Olivier Fléchelles, Moustapha Dramé, Fatiha Najioullah
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒的时间传播表现出明显的地理变异性,特别是在流行病学数据仍然有限的热带地区。了解局部循环模式对于了解温带地区以外的呼吸道病毒 流行病学 的情况至关重要。我们进行了一项单中心、以医院为基础的观察性研究,纳入了 2016 年 1 月至 2021 年 7 月期间在马提尼克大学医院出现急性发热呼吸综合征的患者。通过 RT-PCR 检测了呼吸道样本中的流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、鼻病毒 (RhV) 和人类偏肺病毒 (hMPV)。病毒传播期定义为至少有一次阳性检测的连续几周。共纳入7,316名患者,其中86.2%住院。流感病毒和RSV表现出明显的流行模式,而RhV和hMPV全年都有传播。流感病毒的传播一般发生在11月/12月至4月/5月(平均持续时间14.6±3.0周),而RSV传播则开始较早,从8月/9月至2月/3月(平均持续时间20.0±2.9周)。鼻病毒全年传播,仲夏期间活动减少。2020 年 SARS-CoV-2 的出现与流感病毒和 RSV 循环的严重破坏有关,而 RhV 循环则迅速恢复。这项基于医院的回顾性研究确定了马提尼克岛一家三级转诊医院管理的患者中病毒检测的不同时间模式。这些发现提供了热带加勒比岛屿呼吸道病毒 流行病学 的具体情况数据,可能有助于讨论当地适应的预防策略的时机。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒的时间传播表现出明显的地理变异性,特别是在流行病学数据仍然有限的热带地区。了解局部循环模式对于了解温带地区以外的呼吸道病毒 流行病学 的情况至关重要。
设计与对象
我们进行了一项单中心、以医院为基础的观察性研究,纳入了 2016 年 1 月至 2021 年 7 月期间在马提尼克大学医院出现急性发热呼吸综合征的患者。总共纳入了 7,316 名患者,其中 86.2% 住院治疗。
核心方法
通过 RT-PCR 检测呼吸道样本中的流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV)、鼻病毒 (RhV) 和人偏肺病毒 (hMPV)。病毒传播期定义为至少有一次阳性检测的连续几周。
主要结果
流感病毒和RSV表现出明显的流行模式,而RhV和hMPV全年都有传播。RSV 循环开始较早,从 8 月/9 月到 2 月/3 月(20.0 ± 2.9 周)。2020 年 SARS-CoV-2 的出现与流感病毒和 RSV 循环的严重破坏有关,而 RhV 循环则迅速恢复。
解释与创新
这项基于医院的回顾性研究确定了马提尼克岛一家三级转诊医院管理的患者中病毒检测的不同时间模式。这些发现提供了热带加勒比岛屿呼吸道病毒 流行病学 的具体情况数据,可能有助于讨论当地适应的预防策略的时机。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能会为热带地区针对呼吸道病毒采取适合当地情况的预防策略的时机提供信息。这些数据有助于了解先前信息有限的地区 RSV 和其他呼吸道病毒的 流行病学。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一家三级护理医院进行的,这可能限制了对其他环境或人群的推广。由于样本量大和系统测试,证据强度中等,但单中心设计和回顾性引入了潜在的局限性。

Seasonality of influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus, rhinovirus and metapneumovirus in patients managed at a tertiary care hospital in Martinique (French Caribbean Island), 2016–2021

Authors: Moustapha Agossou, Sloane Rollier, Antoine Enfissi, Laurence Fagour, Dominique Rousset, Janick Jean-Marie, Raymond Césaire, Olivier Fléchelles, Moustapha Dramé, Fatiha Najioullah
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory viruses show marked geographical variability in their temporal circulation, particularly in tropical settings where epidemiological data remain limited. Understanding local circulation patterns is essential to contextualize respiratory virus epidemiology outside temperate regions. We conducted a monocentric, hospital-based observational study including patients presenting with acute febrile respiratory syndromes at the University Hospital of Martinique between January 2016 and July 2021. Respiratory samples were tested by RT-PCR for influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), rhinovirus (RhV), and human metapneumovirus (hMPV). Viral circulation periods were defined as consecutive weeks with at least one positive detection. A total of 7,316 patients were included, of whom 86.2% were hospitalized. Influenza virus and RSV showed clear epidemic patterns, whereas RhV and hMPV circulated throughout the year. Influenza virus circulation generally occurred from November/December to April/May (mean duration 14.6 ± 3.0 weeks), while RSV circulation started earlier, from August/September to February/March (20.0 ± 2.9 weeks). Rhinovirus circulated year-round with reduced activity during mid-summer. The emergence of SARS-CoV-2 in 2020 was associated with major disruptions in the circulation of influenza virus and RSV, while RhV circulation rapidly resumed. This retrospective hospital-based study identified distinct temporal patterns of viral detection among patients managed at a tertiary referral hospital in Martinique. These findings provide context-specific data on respiratory virus epidemiology in a tropical Caribbean island and may contribute to discussions on the timing of locally adapted preventive strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viruses show marked geographical variability in their temporal circulation, particularly in tropical settings where epidemiological data remain limited. Understanding local circulation patterns is essential to contextualize respiratory virus epidemiology outside temperate regions.
Design and population
We conducted a monocentric, hospital-based observational study including patients presenting with acute febrile respiratory syndromes at the University Hospital of Martinique between January 2016 and July 2021. A total of 7,316 patients were included, of whom 86.2% were hospitalized.
Core methods
Respiratory samples were tested by RT-PCR for influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), rhinovirus (RhV), and human metapneumovirus (hMPV). Viral circulation periods were defined as consecutive weeks with at least one positive detection.
Main results
Influenza virus and RSV showed clear epidemic patterns, whereas RhV and hMPV circulated throughout the year. RSV circulation started earlier, from August/September to February/March (20.0 ± 2.9 weeks). The emergence of SARS-CoV-2 in 2020 was associated with major disruptions in the circulation of influenza virus and RSV, while RhV circulation rapidly resumed.
Interpretation and novelty
This retrospective hospital-based study identified distinct temporal patterns of viral detection among patients managed at a tertiary referral hospital in Martinique. These findings provide context-specific data on respiratory virus epidemiology in a tropical Caribbean island and may contribute to discussions on the timing of locally adapted preventive strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the timing of locally adapted preventive strategies for respiratory viruses in tropical settings. The data contribute to understanding the epidemiology of RSV and other respiratory viruses in a region with limited previous information.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted at a single tertiary care hospital, which may limit generalizability to other settings or populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size and systematic testing, but the single-center design and retrospective nature introduce potential limitations.

婴儿感染 呼吸道合胞病毒 基因型和随后的儿童哮喘风险。

Infant Infection With Respiratory Syncytial Virus Genotypes and Subsequent Childhood Asthma Risk.
作者： Christian Rosas-Salazar, Tebeb Gebretsadik, James D Chappell, R Stokes Peebles, William D Dupont, Meghan H Shilts, Samadhan J Jadhao, Larry J Anderson, Suman R Das, Tina V Hartert
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查婴儿期 RSV 感染(含有先前报道的 G 基因序列重复 (Gdups) 的基因型)是否与儿童哮喘风险增加相关。
设计与对象
对健康足月儿童进行了一项基于人群的前瞻性出生 队列研究 研究,以评估 RSV 感染与儿童哮喘风险之间的关联。
核心方法
该研究涉及分析出生 队列研究 的健康足月儿童的 RSV 感染情况,特别关注含有 G 基因序列重复 (Gdups) 的基因型及其与哮喘结果的关系。
主要结果
与未感染 RSV-A Gdup+ 的儿童相比,RSV-A Gdup+ 感染的儿童 5 年当前哮喘的调整后比值比为 2.00 (95%CI = 1.15-3.47),而 RSV-B Gdup+ 感染的调整后比值比为 1.78 (95%CI = 1.01-3.11)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,RSV 的遗传变异,特别是 G 基因重复,可能会导致儿童哮喘风险的变化,为这种关联提供了概念验证证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来的 监测 和 RSV 遗传变异及其在儿童哮喘发展中的潜在作用的研究工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其关注的是健康足月儿童,这可能会影响普遍性,而且证据基于观察数据,导致研究结果的可信度中等。

Infant Infection With Respiratory Syncytial Virus Genotypes and Subsequent Childhood Asthma Risk.

Authors: Christian Rosas-Salazar, Tebeb Gebretsadik, James D Chappell, R Stokes Peebles, William D Dupont, Meghan H Shilts, Samadhan J Jadhao, Larry J Anderson, Suman R Das, Tina V Hartert
Original abstract (English)
Our objective was to test the hypothesis that respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection during infancy with genotypes containing previously reported G gene sequence duplications (Gdups) is associated with an increased risk of childhood asthma. In a population-based, prospective, birth cohort of healthy, term children, we found that children with RSV-A Gdup+ (adjusted odds ratio [aOR] = 2.00, 95%CI = 1.15-3.47) and RSV-B Gdup + (aOR = 1.78, 95%CI = 1.01-3.11) infections during infancy had higher odds of 5-year current asthma than those without RSV infection during infancy. These findings are proof-of-concept evidence that RSV genetic variation is associated with variable childhood asthma risk.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether RSV infection during infancy with genotypes containing previously reported G gene sequence duplications (Gdups) is associated with an increased risk of childhood asthma.
Design and population
A population-based, prospective, birth cohort study of healthy, term children was conducted to assess the association between RSV infection and childhood asthma risk.
Core methods
The study involved analyzing RSV infections in a birth cohort of healthy, term children, with specific attention to genotypes containing G gene sequence duplications (Gdups) and their association with asthma outcomes.
Main results
Children with RSV-A Gdup+ infections had an adjusted odds ratio of 2.00 (95%CI = 1.15-3.47) for 5-year current asthma compared to those without RSV infection, while RSV-B Gdup+ infections had an adjusted odds ratio of 1.78 (95%CI = 1.01-3.11).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that genetic variation in RSV, specifically G gene duplications, may contribute to variable asthma risk in children, providing proof-of-concept evidence for this association.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future surveillance and research efforts on RSV genetic variation and its potential role in childhood asthma development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on healthy, term children, which may affect generalizability, and the evidence is based on observational data, resulting in moderate confidence in the findings.

槐生物碱的抗人呼吸道合胞病毒活性评价及脂质体气管内给药增强功效的评价

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration
作者： Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
摘要中文翻译人类 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是婴儿、幼儿和老年人严重下呼吸道感染和相关死亡的主要原因。由于 RSV 的高 突变 发生率以及现有 抗病毒药物 药物的疗效和安全性有限,RSV 的临床管理具有挑战性。苦参生物碱是从苦参中提取的天然化合物,具有广谱 抗病毒药物 和免疫调节活性。从机制上讲,RSV依赖ERK1/2磷酸化来促进病毒复制和传播,而槐花生物碱通过调节MEK/ERK信号通路抑制ERK1/2磷酸化,从而抑制病毒增殖和RSV感染引起的炎症反应。体内研究表明,槐花生物碱通过气管内给药可显着降低 RSV 感染小鼠的 死亡率。为了进一步增强药物的肺滞留,采用脂质体来提高苦参生物碱的治疗效果。槐果碱(SC)作为抗RSV感染最有效的生物碱,将其封装在脂质体中以增强其抗RSV感染的效果。体内研究表明,在 RSV 感染的小鼠模型中,负载 SC 的脂质体在抑制 RSV 复制和减轻肺部炎症方面优于游离 SC。本研究不仅阐明了槐花生物碱抗RSV感染的机制,而且提供了SC的脂质体制剂,以提高抗RSV的治疗效果。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估槐树生物碱的抗人 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 活性,并探索通过脂质体递送增强其功效的方法。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 RSV 感染小鼠进行的体内实验,重点是评估槐树生物碱及其脂质体制剂的治疗效果。
核心方法
测试了槐生物碱抑制 ERK1/2 磷酸化(RSV 复制的关键机制)的能力,并使用脂质体来改善药物保留和治疗结果。
主要结果
体内研究表明,槐花生物碱显着减少了RSV感染的小鼠死亡率,并且负载SC的脂质体比游离SC更能有效地抑制RSV复制和减轻肺部炎症。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,槐花生物碱通过 MEK/ERK 信号通路抑制 RSV 复制,而脂质体递送增强了其治疗潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果为 RSV 新型 抗病毒药物 疗法的开发提供了见解,可能会改善严重呼吸道感染的 治疗 选择。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体内模型,证据基于单一实验设置,这可能会影响普遍性。证据强度中等。

An evaluation of the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus activity of sophora alkaloids and the enhanced efficacy by liposomes via intratracheal administration

Authors: Guikang Wang, Ke Zhang, Lei Huang, Chunyuan Liu, Shaoju Hu, Yusi Luo, Zipeng Gong, Jianqing Peng
Original abstract (English)
Human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a major cause of severe lower respiratory tract infections and related deaths in infants, young children, and the elderly. The clinical management of RSV is challenging due to its high mutation rate and the limited efficacy and safety of existing antiviral drugs. Sophora alkaloids, natural compounds derived from Sophora flavescens, exhibit broad-spectrum antiviral and immunomodulatory activities. Mechanistically, RSV relies on ERK1/2 phosphorylation to facilitate viral replication and spread, while sophora alkaloids inhibit ERK1/2 phosphorylation via regulating MEK/ERK signaling pathway, thereby suppressing viral proliferation and the inflammatory response caused by RSV infection. In vivo studies demonstrated that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced the mortality of RSV-infected mice via intratracheal administration. To further enhance the pulmonary retention of drugs, liposomes were used to improve the therapeutic effects of sophora alkaloids. As the most effective sophora alkaloids against RSV infection, sophocarpine (SC) was encapsulated in liposomes to enhance its anti-RSV infection effect. In vivo studies demonstrate that SC-loaded liposomes outperformed the free SC in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation in murine models of RSV infection. This study not only clarifies the mechanism by which sophora alkaloids counteract RSV-infection but also provides liposomal formulation of SC to improve the anti-RSV therapeutic effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the anti-human respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) activity of sophora alkaloids and explore ways to enhance their efficacy through liposomal delivery.
Design and population
The study involved in vivo experiments using RSV-infected mice, with a focus on assessing the therapeutic effects of sophora alkaloids and their liposomal formulations.
Core methods
Sophora alkaloids were tested for their ability to inhibit ERK1/2 phosphorylation, a key mechanism in RSV replication, and liposomes were used to improve drug retention and therapeutic outcomes.
Main results
In vivo studies showed that sophora alkaloids significantly reduced RSV-infected mice mortality, and SC-loaded liposomes were more effective in suppressing RSV replication and alleviating pulmonary inflammation than free SC.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that sophora alkaloids inhibit RSV replication through the MEK/ERK signaling pathway, and liposomal delivery enhances their therapeutic potential.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results provide insights into the development of novel antiviral therapies for RSV, potentially improving treatment options for severe respiratory infections.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo models, and the evidence is based on a single experimental setup, which may affect generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate.
