C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

澳大利亚 2025 年 RSV 混合免疫计划的影响和有效性:PAEDS-FluCAN 网络的结果

Impact and effectiveness of Australia's 2025 hybrid RSV immunisation program: results from the PAEDS-FluCAN Network
作者： Ushma Wadia, Peter C. Richmond, Philip N. Britton, Jeremy Carr, Julia E. Clark, Nigel W. Crawford, Te-Yu Hung, Helen S. Marshall, Kristine K. Macartney, Nicholas J. Wood
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
澳大利亚于 2025 年推出了混合 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 母婴保护计划 (RSV-MIPP),使用国家免疫计划资助的 RSV 疫苗(针对妊娠 28 周以上的孕妇)和司法管辖区资助的 尼塞韦单抗。
设计与对象
我们在 13 家 PAEDS-FluCAN 网络医院进行了一项全国性前瞻性检测阴性病例对照研究,提供了儿科 RSV 数据。从2024年4月到2025年11月,招募了患有RSV相关急性呼吸道感染(ARI;病例)的住院儿童和同期RSV阴性对照。
核心方法
使用多变量回归测试阴性设计估计免疫效果,调整预先指定的协变量,并计算为(1−调整优势比)× 100%,置信区间(CI)为 95%。影响评估为 2024 年至 2025 年流行病学第 14 至 48 周内 RSV 住院累计人数的变化,不包括两个州实施综合 2024 尼塞韦单抗 计划的医院,并按年龄组进行分层。
主要结果
2025年1月1日至11月30日期间,招募了3743名儿童(2719名RSV阳性病例;1024名测试阴性对照)。RSV-MIPP 新生儿 队列研究(2 月 17 日出生)针对 RSV 相关 ARI 住院治疗的总体有效性为 82·6%(95% CI:70·6、89·7)。该计划的影响基于单个预计划比较年并排除 2 个州,与 0-<3 个月、3-<6 个月和 6-12 个月婴儿的住院率分别减少 43·8%、20·1% 和 8·5% 相关。
解释与创新
这些对南半球混合 RSV 预防计划的首次估计表明,在一个季节内对 RSV 住院治疗具有很高的有效性。需要对 RSV-MIPP 进行纵向评估,以了解持续的有效性和全年人口水平的影响。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持混合 RSV 免疫计划在减少婴儿住院方面的有效性,为政策制定者和公共卫生官员提供证据,以告知未来的 疫苗接种 战略和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
由于预计划比较年份单一,并且排除了两个具有综合 尼塞韦单抗 计划的州,因此证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性。研究设计和统计方法是稳健的,但需要纵向评估来确认持续有效性。

Impact and effectiveness of Australia's 2025 hybrid RSV immunisation program: results from the PAEDS-FluCAN Network

Authors: Ushma Wadia, Peter C. Richmond, Philip N. Britton, Jeremy Carr, Julia E. Clark, Nigel W. Crawford, Te-Yu Hung, Helen S. Marshall, Kristine K. Macartney, Nicholas J. Wood
Original abstract (English)
Background Australia introduced a hybrid Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Maternal and Infant Protection Program (RSV-MIPP) in 2025, using National Immunisation Program-funded RSV vaccine for pregnant people from 28 weeks' gestation and jurisdiction-funded nirsevimab. We estimated real-world effectiveness and assessed the impact of the RSV-MIPP program across a participating surveillance network. Methods We conducted a national, prospective test-negative case–control study at thirteen PAEDS-FluCAN network hospitals contributing paediatric RSV data. From April 2024 to November 2025, hospitalised children with RSV-associated acute respiratory infection (ARI; cases) and contemporaneous RSV-negative controls were recruited. Immunisation effectiveness was estimated using a test-negative design with multivariable regression, adjusted for prespecified covariates, and calculated as (1−adjusted odds ratio) × 100% with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Impact was assessed as the 2024–2025 change in the cumulative number of RSV hospitalisations during epidemiological weeks 14–48, excluding hospitals in two states with comprehensive 2024 nirsevimab programs, and stratified by age group. Findings Between 1 January and 30 November 2025, 3743 children were recruited (2719 RSV-positive cases; 1024 test-negative controls). Overall effectiveness against RSV-associated ARI hospitalisation in the RSV-MIPP newborn cohort (born from 17 February) was 82·6% (95% CI: 70·6, 89·7). Effectiveness estimates for maternal vaccination and nirsevimab were 81·5% (68·4, 89·2) and 89·6% (73·6, 95·9) respectively. Nirsevimab effectiveness in the catch-up cohort (born 1st October 2024–16th February 2025) was 89·6% (66·4, 96·8). The program's impact, based on a single preprogram comparison year and excluding 2 states, was associated with 43·8%, 20·1% and 8·5% reduction in hospitalisations in infants aged 0–<3 months, 3–<6 months and 6–12 months respectively. Interpretation These first southern hemisphere estimates of a hybrid RSV prevention program demonstrated high effectiveness against RSV hospitalisation over a single season. Longitudinal evaluation of the RSV-MIPP is needed to understand sustained effectiveness and year-round population level impacts. Funding This work was sponsored by the Australian Government Department of Health, Disability and Ageing and the Australian Centre for Disease Control.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Australia introduced a hybrid Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) Maternal and Infant Protection Program (RSV-MIPP) in 2025, using National Immunisation Program-funded RSV vaccine for pregnant people from 28 weeks' gestation and jurisdiction-funded nirsevimab.
Design and population
We conducted a national, prospective test-negative case–control study at thirteen PAEDS-FluCAN network hospitals contributing paediatric RSV data. From April 2024 to November 2025, hospitalised children with RSV-associated acute respiratory infection (ARI; cases) and contemporaneous RSV-negative controls were recruited.
Core methods
Immunisation effectiveness was estimated using a test-negative design with multivariable regression, adjusted for prespecified covariates, and calculated as (1−adjusted odds ratio) × 100% with 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Impact was assessed as the 2024–2025 change in the cumulative number of RSV hospitalisations during epidemiological weeks 14–48, excluding hospitals in two states with comprehensive 2024 nirsevimab programs, and stratified by age group.
Main results
Between 1 January and 30 November 2025, 3743 children were recruited (2719 RSV-positive cases; 1024 test-negative controls). Overall effectiveness against RSV-associated ARI hospitalisation in the RSV-MIPP newborn cohort (born from 17 February) was 82·6% (95% CI: 70·6, 89·7). The program's impact, based on a single preprogram comparison year and excluding 2 states, was associated with 43·8%, 20·1% and 8·5% reduction in hospitalisations in infants aged 0–<3 months, 3–<6 months and 6–12 months respectively.
Interpretation and novelty
These first southern hemisphere estimates of a hybrid RSV prevention program demonstrated high effectiveness against RSV hospitalisation over a single season. Longitudinal evaluation of the RSV-MIPP is needed to understand sustained effectiveness and year-round population level impacts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the effectiveness of a hybrid RSV immunisation program in reducing hospitalisations in infants, providing evidence for policy makers and public health officials to inform future vaccination strategies and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the single preprogram comparison year and exclusion of two states with comprehensive nirsevimab programs, which may limit generalisability. The study design and statistical methods are robust, but longitudinal evaluation is needed to confirm sustained effectiveness.

Journal: Nanoscale horizons  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-03  |  DOI: 10.1039/d6nh00043f  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 11, 8, 2158-2166
C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

通过观察其时空动态揭示了 RSV F 介导的细胞融合的肌动蛋白依赖性调节。

Actin-dependent regulation of RSV F-mediated cell-cell fusion revealed by visualizing its spatiotemporal dynamics.
作者： Yu Qiu, Xin Ji, Jinrui Zhang, Jing Gao, Yan Shi, Yangang Pan, Guanghua Che, Haijiao Xu, Hongda Wang
摘要中文翻译呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 通过其融合 (F) 蛋白诱导致病性合胞体,但其如何在拥挤的质膜内协调融合仍不清楚。通过整合活细胞和 dSTORM 成像,我们可视化了 RSV F 从分泌到膜组装的时空动态。我们表明,RSV F 有效地运输到质膜并触发富含 F-肌动蛋白的突起,从而促进融合,这取决于分支肌动蛋白的重塑。超分辨率成像进一步揭示RSV F在细胞与细胞接触处从纳米级簇重组为近连续的带状纳米域,形成稳定的融合平台。这项工作直接可视化了病毒融合剂的肌动蛋白驱动的纳米级组装,定义了 RSV 发病机制 的关键机制,并揭示了 抗病毒药物 干预的纳米级目标,从而强调了先进纳米显微镜在分子水平上揭示复杂的 宿主 与病原体相互作用的力量。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在阐明 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 如何通过其融合 (F) 蛋白在拥挤的质膜内协调融合,从而解决 RSV 发病机制 的关键知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究集成了活细胞和 dSTORM 成像,以可视化细胞环境中 RSV F 从分泌到膜组装的时空动态。
核心方法
该研究采用活细胞和 dSTORM 成像技术来追踪 RSV F 运输和组装,并分析富含 F 肌动蛋白的突起和纳米级簇重组。
主要结果
RSV F 有效地运输到质膜并触发富含 F-肌动蛋白的突起,促进融合,依赖于分支肌动蛋白重塑,并在细胞与细胞接触处重组成近连续的带状纳米结构域。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为定义了 RSV 发病机制 中关键的肌动蛋白驱动机制,并揭示了 抗病毒药物 干预的纳米级靶标,凸显了先进纳米显微镜在研究 宿主 与病原体相互作用中的力量。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为针对 RSV F 纳米级组装的潜在 抗病毒药物 策略提供了信息,并证明了先进成像在理解病毒融合机制方面的实用性。
局限与证据强度
由于纳米级动力学的直接可视化,证据强度很高,尽管局限性可能包括报告数据的仅抽象性质,没有完整的方法细节。

Actin-dependent regulation of RSV F-mediated cell-cell fusion revealed by visualizing its spatiotemporal dynamics.

Authors: Yu Qiu, Xin Ji, Jinrui Zhang, Jing Gao, Yan Shi, Yangang Pan, Guanghua Che, Haijiao Xu, Hongda Wang
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) induces pathogenic syncytia via its fusion (F) protein, yet how it orchestrates fusion within the crowded plasma membrane remains unclear. By integrating live-cell and dSTORM imaging, we visualized the spatiotemporal dynamics of RSV F from secretion to membrane assembly. We show that RSV F efficiently traffics to the plasma membrane and triggers F-actin-enriched protrusions that facilitate fusion, depending on branched actin remodeling. Super-resolution imaging further reveals that RSV F reorganizes from nanoscale clusters into near-continuous ribbon-like nanodomains at cell-cell contacts, forming a stable fusion platform. This work directly visualizes the actin-driven nanoscale assembly of a viral fusogen, defining a key mechanism in RSV pathogenesis and revealing a nanoscale target for antiviral intervention, thereby underscoring the power of advanced nanoscopy to unravel complex host-pathogen interactions at the molecular level.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to elucidate how respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) orchestrates fusion within the crowded plasma membrane via its fusion (F) protein, addressing a key knowledge gap in RSV pathogenesis.
Design and population
The research integrated live-cell and dSTORM imaging to visualize the spatiotemporal dynamics of RSV F from secretion to membrane assembly in a cellular context.
Core methods
The study employed live-cell and dSTORM imaging techniques to track RSV F trafficking and assembly, along with analysis of F-actin-enriched protrusions and nanoscale cluster reorganization.
Main results
RSV F efficiently trafficked to the plasma membrane and triggered F-actin-enriched protrusions facilitating fusion, dependent on branched actin remodeling, and reorganized into near-continuous ribbon-like nanodomains at cell-cell contacts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as defining a key actin-driven mechanism in RSV pathogenesis and revealing a nanoscale target for antiviral intervention, highlighting the power of advanced nanoscopy in studying host-pathogen interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform potential antiviral strategies targeting the nanoscale assembly of RSV F and demonstrate the utility of advanced imaging for understanding viral fusion mechanisms.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is high due to direct visualization of nanoscale dynamics, though limitations may include the abstract-only nature of the reported data without full methodological details.

三级护理中心急性呼吸道病毒感染儿科患者的呼吸道病毒负担和医疗保健利用情况。

Respiratory Viral Burden and Healthcare Utilization Among Pediatric Patients With Acute Respiratory Viral Infections in a Tertiary Care Center.
作者： Shafiqa Alsharif
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 呼吸道病毒感染,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和流感,是冬季儿科发病、住院和医疗压力的主要原因。本研究旨在评估儿科患者 RSV 和流感的临床和流行病学负担,并评估相关的医疗资源利用率。方法 这项回顾性 队列研究 研究分析了 1428 名各个年龄段的患者,包括 2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 3 月期间到沙特阿拉伯三级护理中心急诊科就诊的儿科亚组(0-14 岁)。使用快速抗原测试检测 SARS-CoV-2、甲型/乙型流感、RSV 和腺病毒,检测病毒病原体。进行描述性流行病学分析以评估临床趋势和医疗保健利用。结果 共有 1428 名患者接受了检测,其中 257 名患者(18%)确诊呼吸道病毒阳性。儿科组 (n = 158) 的平均年龄为 3.2 岁,主要由幼儿组成,其中 48% 为 2 岁以下,76% 为 5 岁以下。以 RSV (37%) 和甲型流感 (36%) 为主,其次是乙型流感 (14%)、SARS-CoV-2 (7.0%) 和腺病毒 (5.1%)。阳性率高峰出现在 1 月份(检测到的病例的 34%),并持续检测到 3 月份(15%)。大多数儿童(60%)已出院,31% 需要住院治疗;入住 ICU 的情况并不常见。12%(n = 19)需要氧疗,7 名患者接受了 抗病毒药物 治疗(奥司他韦)。高级呼吸支持包括高流量鼻插管 (HFNC;n = 11) 和无创通气(持续气道正压通气 [CPAP] 和双水平气道正压通气 [BiPAP];n = 12),凸显了巨大的医疗保健需求。结论 这项研究表明,冬季 RSV 和流感对幼儿造成了巨大的负担,导致急诊就诊、入院和呼吸支持使用率较高。药物需求的增加凸显了主动规划、资源分配和预防策略的必要性,以有效管理季节性医疗压力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道病毒感染,特别是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和流感,是冬季儿科发病、住院和医疗压力的主要原因。本研究旨在评估儿科患者 RSV 和流感的临床和流行病学负担,并评估相关的医疗资源利用率。
设计与对象
这项回顾性 队列研究 研究分析了 1428 名各个年龄段的患者,其中包括 2024 年 10 月至 2025 年 3 月期间到沙特阿拉伯三级护理中心急诊科就诊的儿科亚组(0-14 岁)。儿科组(n = 158)的平均年龄为 3.2 岁,主要由幼儿组成,其中 48% 2 岁以下,76% 5 岁以下。

主要结果
共有 1428 名患者接受了检测,其中 257 名(18%)确诊呼吸道病毒呈阳性。RSV (37%) 和甲型流感 (36%) 占主导地位,其次是乙型流感 (14%)、SARS-CoV-2 (7.0%) 和腺病毒 (5.1%)。阳性率高峰出现在 1 月份(检测到的病例的 34%),并持续检测到 3 月份(15%)。大多数儿童(60%)已出院,31% 需要住院治疗;入住 ICU 的情况并不常见。
解释与创新
这项研究表明,冬季 RSV 和流感对幼儿造成了巨大的负担,导致急诊就诊、入院和呼吸支持使用率较高。RSV 和甲型流感的主导地位凸显了它们在季节性儿科呼吸道感染中的重要作用。
科研与公卫意义
药物需求的增加凸显了主动规划、资源分配和预防策略的必要性,以有效管理季节性医疗压力。研究结果强调了针对儿童群体中 RSV 和流感的针对性 监测 和干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和单中心设置而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。尽管存在这些局限性,但大样本量和详细的临床数据为儿科患者的 RSV 和流感负担提供了适度的证据。

Respiratory Viral Burden and Healthcare Utilization Among Pediatric Patients With Acute Respiratory Viral Infections in a Tertiary Care Center.

Authors: Shafiqa Alsharif
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Respiratory viral infections, particularly Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) and influenza, are major causes of pediatric morbidity, hospitalization, and healthcare strain during winter seasons. This study aims to evaluate the clinical and epidemiological burden of RSV and influenza in pediatric patients and assess associated healthcare resource utilization. Methods This retrospective cohort study analyzed 1428 patients of all ages, including a pediatric subgroup (0–14 years) who presented to an emergency department in a tertiary care center in Saudi Arabia between October 2024 and March 2025. Viral pathogens were detected using a rapid antigen test detecting SARS‐CoV‐2, influenza A/B, RSV, and adenovirus. Descriptive epidemiological analysis was performed to assess clinical trends and healthcare utilization. Results A total of 1428 patients were tested, of whom 257 (18%) confirmed positive for respiratory viruses. The pediatric group ( n = 158) had a mean age of 3.2 years and was largely composed of young children, with 48% under 2 years and 76% under 5. RSV (37%) and Influenza A (36%) predominated, followed by Influenza B (14%), SARS‐CoV‐2 (7.0%), and Adenovirus (5.1%). Peak positivity occurred in January (34% of detected cases) with continued detections into March (15%). Most children (60%) were discharged, while 31% required hospitalization; ICU admissions were infrequent. Oxygen therapy was required in 12% ( n = 19), and antiviral therapy (oseltamivir) was administered in 7 patients. Advanced respiratory support included high‐flow nasal cannula (HFNC; n = 11) and non‐invasive ventilation (continuous positive airway pressure [CPAP] and bilevel positive airway pressure [BiPAP]; n = 12), highlighting substantial healthcare demands. Conclusion This study shows a substantial winter burden from RSV and influenza in young children, leading to high emergency visits, admissions, and respiratory support use. Increased medication demand highlights the need for proactive planning, resource allocation, and preventive strategies to manage seasonal healthcare pressures effectively.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory viral infections, particularly Respiratory Syncytial Virus (RSV) and influenza, are major causes of pediatric morbidity, hospitalization, and healthcare strain during winter seasons. This study aims to evaluate the clinical and epidemiological burden of RSV and influenza in pediatric patients and assess associated healthcare resource utilization.
Design and population
This retrospective cohort study analyzed 1428 patients of all ages, including a pediatric subgroup (0–14 years) who presented to an emergency department in a tertiary care center in Saudi Arabia between October 2024 and March 2025. The pediatric group (n = 158) had a mean age of 3.2 years and was largely composed of young children, with 48% under 2 years and 76% under 5.
Core methods
Viral pathogens were detected using a rapid antigen test detecting SARS‐CoV‐2, influenza A/B, RSV, and adenovirus. Descriptive epidemiological analysis was performed to assess clinical trends and healthcare utilization.
Main results
A total of 1428 patients were tested, of whom 257 (18%) confirmed positive for respiratory viruses. RSV (37%) and Influenza A (36%) predominated, followed by Influenza B (14%), SARS‐CoV‐2 (7.0%), and Adenovirus (5.1%). Peak positivity occurred in January (34% of detected cases) with continued detections into March (15%). Most children (60%) were discharged, while 31% required hospitalization; ICU admissions were infrequent.
Interpretation and novelty
This study shows a substantial winter burden from RSV and influenza in young children, leading to high emergency visits, admissions, and respiratory support use. The predominance of RSV and Influenza A highlights their significant role in seasonal pediatric respiratory infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
Increased medication demand highlights the need for proactive planning, resource allocation, and preventive strategies to manage seasonal healthcare pressures effectively. The findings underscore the importance of targeted surveillance and intervention for RSV and influenza in pediatric populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and single-center setting, which may affect generalizability. Despite these limitations, the large sample size and detailed clinical data provide moderate evidence for the burden of RSV and influenza in pediatric patients.

意大利米兰的呼吸道病毒综合医院、急诊科和社区 监测。

Integrated Hospital, Emergency Department, and Community Surveillance for Respiratory Viruses in Milan, Italy.
作者： Alberto Rizzo, Federica Salari, Cristina Galli, Manuel Maffeo, Cristina Paduraru, Simone Villa, Alberto Dolci, Elena Pariani, Danilo Cereda, Milan Respiratory Viruses Surveillance Working Group
摘要中文翻译呼吸道病毒的传播可能因医疗机构和年龄组的不同而有所不同,这可能会限制基于单一来源的 监测 的解释性。在意大利米兰,已建立的基于社区和急诊科的系统与医院实验室检测并存,但医院病房级数据并未定期整合。本研究的目的是描述呼吸道病毒的 流行病学 并评估基于病房的 监测 是否与社区和 ED 监测 相补充。我们分析了 2024 年 1 月 1 日(W1/2024)至 2025 年 10 月 12 日(W41/2025)在社区、急诊室和医院病房收集的呼吸道样本的分子检测结果。病毒包括甲型流感 (IAV)、乙型流感 (IBV)、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2(其中包含扩展组)。在 8029 个样本中,≥ 1 种病毒的总体阳性率为 46.8%,急诊科 (51.1%) 和社区 (50.5%) 最高,病房较低 (36.7%) (p < 0.001)。2024-2025年期间,IAV首先在病房(W33/2024)中检测到,并且在病房(W2/2025)中比社区(W3/2025)和急诊室(W4/2025)更早达到峰值。IBV 首次在急诊科 (W36/2024) 中检测到,并在急诊科 (W5/2025)、社区 (W7/2025) 和病房 (W8/2025) 中达到高峰。RSV 首先在社区 (W37/2024) 中重新出现,并在社区/急诊科 (W50/2024) 中达到顶峰,随后在病房 (W2/2025) 中达到顶峰。不同医疗机构和年龄组的呼吸道病毒阳性率和描述性高峰时间有所不同。以病房为基础的 监测 为住院和老年人群提供了补充信息,但它并不总是比社区或 ED 监测 早或优于。当与测试人群、测试适应症和临床结果可用性的差异一起解释时,集成的多设置 监测 可以提高态势感知。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
单一来源的 监测 可能无法完全捕获跨医疗机构和年龄组的呼吸道病毒传播模式,从而为公共卫生行动造成可解释性差距。
设计与对象
该研究整合了 2024 年 1 月 1 日至 2025 年 10 月 12 日在意大利米兰社区、急诊室 (ED) 和医院病房收集的呼吸道样本的分子检测结果。
核心方法
对样本进行了呼吸道病毒检测,包括甲型流感、乙型流感、RSV 和 SARS-CoV-2,并在部分病例中使用了扩展检测组。
主要结果
8029 个样本中总体病毒阳性率为 46.8%,急诊科 (51.1%) 和社区 (50.5%) 最高,病房较低 (36.7%) (p < 0.001)。RSV 首先在社区中再次出现(第 37/2024 周),并在病房(第 2/2025 周)之前在社区/急诊室(第 50/2024 周)达到顶峰。
解释与创新
病房 监测 为住院和老年人群提供了补充信息,但在检测 RSV 循环方面并不总是早于或优于社区或 ED 监测。
科研与公卫意义
集成的多设置 监测 可以通过考虑不同设置中测试人群、测试适应症和临床结果可用性的差异来增强 RSV 的态势感知。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于常规 监测 数据,没有标准化的测试指征或结果联系,这可能会影响研究结果的可比性和普遍性。

Integrated Hospital, Emergency Department, and Community Surveillance for Respiratory Viruses in Milan, Italy.

Authors: Alberto Rizzo, Federica Salari, Cristina Galli, Manuel Maffeo, Cristina Paduraru, Simone Villa, Alberto Dolci, Elena Pariani, Danilo Cereda, Milan Respiratory Viruses Surveillance Working Group
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory virus circulation may vary across healthcare settings and age groups, potentially limiting the interpretability of surveillance based on a single source. In Milan, Italy, established community- and emergency department-based systems coexist with hospital laboratory testing, but hospital ward-level data are not routinely integrated. The aims of this study were to describe respiratory viruses' epidemiology and assess whether ward-based surveillance complements community and ED surveillance. We analyzed molecular test results from respiratory samples collected from January 1, 2024 (W1/2024) to October 12, 2025 (W41/2025) in community, ED, and hospital ward settings. Viruses included influenza A (IAV), influenza B (IBV), RSV, and SARS-CoV-2 (with extended panels in a subset). Among 8029 samples, overall positivity for ≥ 1 virus was 46.8%, highest in ED (51.1%) and community (50.5%) and lower in wards (36.7%) (p < 0.001). During 2024-2025, IAV was first detected in wards (W33/2024) and peaked earlier in wards (W2/2025) than community (W3/2025) and ED (W4/2025). IBV was first detected in ED (W36/2024) and peaked in ED (W5/2025), community (W7/2025), and wards (W8/2025). RSV reappeared first in community (W37/2024) and peaked in community/ED (W50/2024) and later in wards (W2/2025). Respiratory virus positivity and descriptive peak timing differed across healthcare settings and age groups. Ward-based surveillance provided complementary information for hospitalized and older populations, but it was not consistently earlier or superior to community or ED surveillance. Integrated, multi-setting surveillance can improve situational awareness when interpreted alongside differences in tested populations, testing indications, and clinical outcome availability.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory virus circulation patterns across healthcare settings and age groups may be incompletely captured by single-source surveillance, creating interpretability gaps for public health action.
Design and population
The study integrated molecular test results from respiratory samples collected in Milan, Italy, from January 1, 2024, to October 12, 2025, across community, emergency department (ED), and hospital ward settings.
Core methods
Samples were tested for respiratory viruses including influenza A, influenza B, RSV, and SARS-CoV-2, with extended panels used in a subset of cases.
Main results
Overall virus positivity was 46.8% among 8029 samples, highest in ED (51.1%) and community (50.5%) and lower in wards (36.7%) (p < 0.001). RSV reappeared first in the community (week 37/2024) and peaked in community/ED (week 50/2024) before wards (week 2/2025).
Interpretation and novelty
Ward-based surveillance provided complementary information for hospitalized and older populations but was not consistently earlier or superior to community or ED surveillance in detecting RSV circulation.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrated, multi-setting surveillance can enhance situational awareness for RSV by accounting for differences in tested populations, testing indications, and clinical outcome availability across settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on routine surveillance data without standardized testing indications or outcome linkage, which may affect comparability and generalizability of findings.

肾移植受者和透析患者与健康对照者的 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 反应。

RSVPreF3OA vaccine responses in kidney transplant recipients and dialysis patients versus healthy controls.
作者： Hector Rincon-Arevalo, Fabian Halleck, Dorottya Böger, Elias Rosenlehner, Ana-Luisa Stefanski, Caterina Carli, Parthenopi Despina Avaniadi, Friederike Bachmann, Léa-Sophie Dreveton, Anna Henning
摘要中文翻译背景:呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致弱势群体出现严重呼吸道疾病,包括肾移植受者 (KTR) 和透析患者 (DP)。尽管基于 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白的 疫苗 可用,但 KTR 和 DP 中 疫苗 特异性 B 和 T 细胞的免疫表型仍然有限。我们假设与健康对照(HC)相比,RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 在 KTR 和 DP 中诱导不同的免疫特征,反映了免疫抑制和尿毒症对 疫苗 诱导的 B 和 T 细胞反应的影响。方法:在这项观察性 队列研究 中,我们分析了 23 个 KTR、14 个 DP 和 24 个 HC 中常规 队列研究 和基于 F 蛋白的佐剂预融合 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 后的体液和细胞反应。在基线以及 疫苗接种 后第 30 天和第 90 天收集血液和唾液。通过酶联免疫吸附测定和光谱流式细胞术对 RSV 特异性血清免疫球蛋白 G/免疫球蛋白 A、唾液抗体以及抗原特异性 B 和 T 细胞进行定量,同时评估破伤风和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 特异性反应。结果:在 KTR 中,疫苗接种 诱导血清和唾液抗体增加,RSV 特异性 B 细胞扩增,富集 RSV 特异性 B 细胞特有的转换记忆表型,并诱导 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 特异性 T 细胞产生效应细胞因子。这些特征与 HC 中观察到的特征相似,而 DP 中的反应则延迟和减弱。在 队列研究 中,RSV 特异性 B 和 T 细胞表现出相似的表型和功能特征。结论:本研究提供了 90 天内 KTR、DP 和 HC 中 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 反应的详细免疫表型,表明 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗接种 在 KTR 中诱导与 HC 相当的保护性 RSV 反应,而 DP 的细胞和体液反应稍低。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 会导致弱势群体出现严重呼吸道疾病,包括肾移植受者 (KTR) 和透析患者 (DP)。尽管基于 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白的 疫苗 可用,但 KTR 和 DP 中 疫苗 特异性 B 和 T 细胞的免疫表型仍然有限。
设计与对象
在这个观察性 队列研究 中,我们分析了 23 个 KTR、14 个 DP 和 24 个健康对照 (HC) 中按照常规 疫苗接种 和基于 F 蛋白的佐剂预融合 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 的体液和细胞反应。
核心方法
在基线以及 疫苗接种 后第 30 天和第 90 天收集血液和唾液。通过酶联免疫吸附测定和光谱流式细胞术对 RSV 特异性血清免疫球蛋白 G/免疫球蛋白 A、唾液抗体以及抗原特异性 B 和 T 细胞进行定量,同时评估破伤风和严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 特异性反应。
主要结果
在 KTR 中,疫苗接种 诱导血清和唾液抗体增加,RSV 特异性 B 细胞扩增,富集 RSV 特异性 B 细胞特有的转换记忆表型,并诱导 RSV-A 和 RSV-B 特异性 T 细胞产生效应细胞因子。这些特征与 HC 中观察到的特征相似,而 DP 中的反应则延迟和减弱。在 队列研究 中,RSV 特异性 B 和 T 细胞表现出相似的表型和功能特征。
解释与创新
这项研究提供了 90 天内 KTR、DP 和 HC 中 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗 反应的详细免疫表型,表明 RSVPreF3OA 疫苗接种 在 KTR 中诱导与 HC 相当的保护性 RSV 反应,而 DP 的细胞和体液反应稍低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,RSVPreF3OA 疫苗接种 可能对 KTR 有效,支持其在这一弱势群体中的使用,而 DP 可能需要额外的策略来增强 疫苗 反应。
局限与证据强度
该研究是观察性的,样本量较小,限制了普遍性。然而,详细的免疫表型分析为 疫苗 中 疫苗 反应的差异提供了强有力的证据。

RSVPreF3OA vaccine responses in kidney transplant recipients and dialysis patients versus healthy controls.

Authors: Hector Rincon-Arevalo, Fabian Halleck, Dorottya Böger, Elias Rosenlehner, Ana-Luisa Stefanski, Caterina Carli, Parthenopi Despina Avaniadi, Friederike Bachmann, Léa-Sophie Dreveton, Anna Henning
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes severe respiratory illness in vulnerable populations, including kidney transplant recipients (KTRs) and dialysis patients (DPs). Despite the availability of RSV prefusion F protein-based vaccines, immune phenotyping of vaccine-specific B and T cells in KTRs and DPs remains limited. We hypothesized that RSVPreF3OA vaccination induces distinct immune signatures in KTRs and DPs compared with healthy controls (HCs), reflecting the impact of immunosuppression and uraemia on vaccine-induced B and T cell responses. METHODS: In this observational cohort, we analysed humoral and cellular responses following routine vaccination with the adjuvanted prefusion F protein-based RSVPreF3OA vaccine in 23 KTRs, 14 DPs and 24 HCs. Blood and saliva were collected at baseline and on days 30 and 90 post-vaccination. RSV-specific serum immunoglobulin G/immunoglobulin A, salivary antibodies and antigen-specific B and T cells were quantified by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay and spectral flow cytometry, with tetanus- and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2-specific responses assessed in parallel. RESULTS: In KTRs, vaccination induced increases in serum and salivary antibodies, expansion of RSV-specific B cells with enrichment of a switched memory phenotype unique to RSV-specific B cells and induction of RSV-A- and RSV-B-specific T cells with effector cytokine production. These signatures resembled those observed in HCs, whereas responses in DPs were delayed and attenuated. Across cohorts, RSV-specific B and T cells showed comparable phenotypic and functional characteristics. CONCLUSIONS: This study provides detailed immunophenotyping of RSVPreF3OA vaccine responses in KTRs, DPs and HCs over 90 days, showing that RSVPreF3OA vaccination induces protective RSV responses among KTRs comparable to HCs while DPs mount modestly lower cellular and humoral responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes severe respiratory illness in vulnerable populations, including kidney transplant recipients (KTRs) and dialysis patients (DPs). Despite the availability of RSV prefusion F protein-based vaccines, immune phenotyping of vaccine-specific B and T cells in KTRs and DPs remains limited.
Design and population
In this observational cohort, we analysed humoral and cellular responses following routine vaccination with the adjuvanted prefusion F protein-based RSVPreF3OA vaccine in 23 KTRs, 14 DPs and 24 healthy controls (HCs).
Core methods
Blood and saliva were collected at baseline and on days 30 and 90 post-vaccination. RSV-specific serum immunoglobulin G/immunoglobulin A, salivary antibodies and antigen-specific B and T cells were quantified by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay and spectral flow cytometry, with tetanus- and severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2-specific responses assessed in parallel.
Main results
In KTRs, vaccination induced increases in serum and salivary antibodies, expansion of RSV-specific B cells with enrichment of a switched memory phenotype unique to RSV-specific B cells and induction of RSV-A- and RSV-B-specific T cells with effector cytokine production. Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RSVPreF3OA vaccination may be effective in KTRs, supporting its use in this vulnerable population, while DPs may require additional strategies to enhance vaccine responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is observational with a small sample size, limiting generalisability. However, the detailed immunophenotyping provides strong evidence for differential vaccine responses across cohorts.

释放 RSV 疫苗接种 在日本的价值:使用综合精算宏观经济模型进行投资回报分析

Unlocking the value of RSV vaccination in Japan: a return on investment analysis using an integrated actuarial-macroeconomic model
作者： Eleftherios Zarkadoulas, Asako Akimoto, Deven Chauhan, Yufan Ho, Christianah Kolajo, Nikolaos Kotsopoulos, Kentaro Tajima
摘要中文翻译目标 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在老年人和慢性病患者中引起显着的发病率和 死亡率。本研究进行了成本效益分析 (CBA),以评估 RSV 疫苗接种 与辅助 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白 (RSVPreF3) 疫苗 在日本 60 岁以上老年人和 50-59 岁高危成年人中的社会价值。方法 精算人口预测模型与适合日本环境的完整 CBA 框架相结合,用于估计 10 年时间范围内辅助 RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 与无 疫苗接种 的成本和收益。进行确定性敏感性分析来评估参数不确定性。结果我们的结果表明,针对老年人(包括年龄在 50-59 岁、严重 RSV 疾病风险较高的人群和年龄≥60 岁的人群)的 5 年期 疫苗接种 计划与可观的经济效益相关。在年龄 ≥ 60 岁的老年人中,有或没有无谓损失 (DWL) 的总投资回报 (ROI) 为 3.3,疫苗接种 预计可避免 35,501 万日元的生产性产出损失,节省 1,178,403 万日元的直接医疗费用,并防止货币化质量调整生命年 (QALY) 10,699,729 万日元损失。对于 50 至 59 岁的高风险成年人,没有 DWL 的总投资回报率为 3.7,有 DWL 的总投资回报率为 3.8,生产性产出损失相应减少 17,889 万日元,医疗保健成本减少 10,400 万日元,货币化 QALY 损失减少 266,116 万日元。结论 总体而言,从社会角度来看,日本老年人中的辅助 RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 具有良好的性价比,疫苗 的获取和管理成本预计将被巨大的健康、生产力和更广泛的经济效益所抵消。

目标 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 在老年人和慢性病患者中引起显着的发病率和 死亡率。
设计与对象
本研究进行了成本效益分析 (CBA),以评估 RSV 疫苗接种 与辅助 RSV 预融合 F 蛋白 (RSVPreF3) 疫苗 在日本 60 岁以上老年人和 50-59 岁高危成年人中的社会价值。
核心方法
方法 精算人口预测模型与适合日本环境的完整 CBA 框架相结合,用于估计 10 年时间范围内辅助 RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 与无 疫苗接种 的成本和收益。
主要结果
结果我们的结果表明,针对老年人(包括年龄在 50-59 岁、严重 RSV 疾病风险较高的人群和年龄≥60 岁的人群)的 5 年期 疫苗接种 计划与可观的经济效益相关。
解释与创新
结论 总体而言,从社会角度来看,日本老年人中的辅助 RSVPreF3 疫苗接种 具有良好的性价比,疫苗 的获取和管理成本预计将被巨大的健康、生产力和更广泛的经济效益所抵消。

在年龄 ≥ 60 岁的老年人中,有或没有无谓损失 (DWL) 的总投资回报 (ROI) 为 3.3,疫苗接种 预计可避免 35,501 万日元的生产性产出损失,节省 1,178,403 万日元的直接医疗费用,并防止货币化质量调整生命年 (QALY) 10,699,729 万日元损失。

进行确定性敏感性分析来评估参数不确定性。

Unlocking the value of RSV vaccination in Japan: a return on investment analysis using an integrated actuarial-macroeconomic model

Authors: Eleftherios Zarkadoulas, Asako Akimoto, Deven Chauhan, Yufan Ho, Christianah Kolajo, Nikolaos Kotsopoulos, Kentaro Tajima
Original abstract (English)
Objective Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes significant morbidity and mortality among older adults and individuals with chronic conditions. This study conducted a cost–benefit analysis (CBA) to evaluate the societal value of RSV vaccination with the adjuvanted RSV prefusion F protein (RSVPreF3) vaccine among older adults aged ≥60 years and high-risk adults aged 50–59 years in Japan. Methods An actuarial population projection model, combined with a full CBA framework adapted to the Japanese setting, was used to estimate costs and benefits of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination versus no vaccination over a 10-year time horizon. Deterministic sensitivity analyses were conducted to assess parameter uncertainty. Results Our results indicate that a 5-year vaccination program among older adults, including those aged 50–59 years at higher risk for severe RSV disease and those aged ≥60 years, was associated with substantial economic benefits. Among older adults aged ≥60 years, the total return on investment (ROI) was 3.3 with or without deadweight loss (DWL), with vaccination projected to avert Japanese Yen (JPY) 35,501 M in productive output loss, save JPY 1,178,403 M in direct healthcare costs, and prevent JPY 10,699,729 M in monetized quality-adjusted life-year (QALY) loss. For high-risk adults aged 50–59 years, the total ROI was 3.7 without DWL and 3.8 with DWL, with corresponding reductions of JPY 17,889 M in productive output loss, JPY 10,400 M in healthcare costs, and JPY 266,116 M in monetized QALY loss. Conclusion Overall, adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination among older adults in Japan represents good value for money from a societal perspective, where vaccine acquisition and administration costs are expected to be outweighed by substantial health, productivity, and broader economic benefits.

Objective Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) causes significant morbidity and mortality among older adults and individuals with chronic conditions.
Design and population

2007-2024 年厄瓜多尔 毛细支气管炎 住院季节性的区域、海拔和年龄异质性,以及对 RSV 免疫预防时机的影响

Regional, altitudinal, and age-specific heterogeneity of bronchiolitis hospitalization seasonality in Ecuador, 2007–2024, and implications for RSV immunoprophylaxis timing
作者： Jaime Angamarca-Iguago, Jaen Cagua-Ordóñez, Juan Marcos Parise-Vasco, Doménica de Mora, Mónica Escobar-Naranjo, Alfredo Bruno, Daniel Simancas-Racines, Jaen Cagua-Ordoñez
摘要中文翻译背景 急性 毛细支气管炎 主要由 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 引起,是全球婴儿因下呼吸道疾病住院的主要原因。尼塞韦单抗 和母体 RSVpreF (Abrysvo) 提供约 6 个月的保护,因此相对于季节的时间安排会影响其影响。厄瓜多尔横跨赤道,从海平面到海拔 4,000 m 以上的山谷,正在考虑引入这些产品,但缺乏关于 毛细支气管炎 季节性、海拔模式或大流行后稳定性的地方证据。方法 我们对厄瓜多尔统计研究所 2007 年至 2024 年出院人数进行了全国生态时间序列分析(19,788,097 人出院;224 个州中的 204 个州拥有完整数据并形成了分析小组)。主要结局是 12 个月以下儿童的急性 毛细支气管炎(ICD-10 代码 J21)(全国 n = 23,190 名;小组中的 22,724 名),次要结局是三个 RSV 相关年龄带、5 岁以下的 ICD-10 代码 J21 和两个 RSV 特异性 ICD-10 代码(J12.1 和 J20.5;J12.1 和 J20.5;2018-2024)。K35(阑尾炎)是阴性对照。将循环季节性指标与块引导置信区间 (CI)、逐年分解和补充敏感性分析相结合 [移动流行病方法 (MEM)、Bai-Perron 断裂和滞后气候相关性]。结果 科斯塔(+2.16 个月)、塞拉(+1.22)和亚马逊(+1.91)的婴儿 毛细支气管炎 住院加权循环高峰月发生变化,随海拔高度的变化从 500 m 以下的 +2.19 个月减少到 1,500-3,000 m 的 +0.88 个月。Costa 在 2022 年至 2024 年期间保持稳定;塞拉利昂,更重要的是,亚马逊每年变化很大(亚马逊峰值出现在 3 月至 7 月,n = 37-42/年),因此应谨慎阅读汇总估计值。综合 RSV 编码结果密切跟踪 ICD-10 代码 J21 在 Costa(0.15 个月差异)和 Sierra(0.40)的时间,支持 ICD-10 代码 J21 作为这两个地区合理的 RSV 代理,并且三个区域峰值现在跨越 2 个月。结论 厄瓜多尔 毛细支气管炎 季节性变化在大流行后发生变化,其中以科斯塔最为明显且稳定;Sierra 在 2022 年表现出短暂的扰动,并在 2023-2024 年部分回归,而 Amazonía 的估计仍然不稳定且动力不足。由于塞拉利昂和亚马逊地区的区域估计值不如科斯塔稳定,因此单一全国窗口(尼塞韦单抗 2 月至 4 月;预计 1 月至 5 月分娩的孕妇 RSVpreF)是一个更合理的起点,有待进一步季节的确认,最好是实验室确认的 监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决厄瓜多尔缺乏关于 毛细支气管炎 季节性、海拔模式和大流行后稳定性的地方证据的问题,以便为 RSV 免疫预防时机提供信息,因为急性 毛细支气管炎 主要归因于 RSV,并且是全球婴儿因下呼吸道疾病住院的主要原因。
设计与对象
我们对厄瓜多尔统计研究所 2007 年至 2024 年的出院情况进行了全国生态时间序列分析,使用了 224 个州中具有完整记录的 204 个州的数据,重点关注 12 个月以下儿童的急性 毛细支气管炎(ICD-10 代码 J21)(全国 n = 23,190 例;小组中的 22,724 例),次要结果涵盖RSV 相关年龄范围和 RSV 特定 ICD-10 代码。
核心方法
分析将循环季节性指标与块引导置信区间 (CI)、逐年分解和补充敏感性分析相结合,包括移动流行病方法 (MEM)、Bai-Perron 断裂和滞后气候相关性,并以 K35(阑尾炎)作为阴性对照。
主要结果
科斯塔(+2.16 个月)、塞拉(+1.22)和亚马逊(+1.91)的婴儿 毛细支气管炎 住院加权循环高峰月发生了变化,并随海拔而衰减;科斯塔在 2022 年至 2024 年期间保持稳定,而塞拉利昂和亚马逊表现出逐年变化,复合 RSV 编码结果密切跟踪 ICD-10 代码 J21 在科斯塔和塞拉利昂的时间。
解释与创新
作者解释说,厄瓜多尔的 毛细支气管炎 季节性在大流行后发生了变化,其中科斯塔的变化最为明显和稳定,而塞拉显示出短暂的扰动,而亚马逊的估计仍然不稳定且动力不足,突显了 RSV 季节性的区域和海拔异质性的新颖性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,鉴于各地区估计值的稳定性不同,有待进一步季节的确认和实验室确认的 监测,尼塞韦单抗(2 月至 4 月)和母亲 RSVpreF(预计于 1 月至 5 月分娩的妊娠)的单一国家窗口是免疫预防时机的更合理的起点。
局限与证据强度
由于塞拉利昂和亚马逊地区的逐年变化、亚马逊地区的估计不稳定以及需要谨慎的汇总估计等限制,证据强度中等;该研究的生态设计和对 ICD-10 代码作为 RSV 代理的依赖也存在潜在的局限性。

Regional, altitudinal, and age-specific heterogeneity of bronchiolitis hospitalization seasonality in Ecuador, 2007–2024, and implications for RSV immunoprophylaxis timing

Authors: Jaime Angamarca-Iguago, Jaen Cagua-Ordóñez, Juan Marcos Parise-Vasco, Doménica de Mora, Mónica Escobar-Naranjo, Alfredo Bruno, Daniel Simancas-Racines, Jaen Cagua-Ordoñez
Original abstract (English)
Background Acute bronchiolitis, mainly attributed to respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), is the leading cause of infant hospitalization for lower respiratory tract disease worldwide. Nirsevimab and maternal RSVpreF (Abrysvo) confer about 6 months of protection, so timing relative to the season affects their impact. Ecuador spans the equator from sea level to valleys above 4,000 m and is considering introducing these products, yet lacks subnational evidence on the seasonality of bronchiolitis, altitudinal patterning, or post-pandemic stability. Methods We conducted a national ecological time-series analysis of hospital discharges from the Ecuadorian Statistics Institute, 2007–2024 (19,788,097 discharges; 204 of 224 cantons had complete data and formed the analytic panel). The primary outcome was acute bronchiolitis (ICD-10 code J21) in children younger than 12 months ( n = 23,190 nationally; 22,724 in the panel), with secondary outcomes across three RSV-relevant age bands, ICD-10 code J21 under 5 years, and two RSV-specific ICD-10 codes (J12.1 and J20.5; 2018–2024). K35 (appendicitis) was a negative control. Analyses combined circular seasonality metrics with block-bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs), year-by-year disaggregation, and complementary sensitivity analyses [Moving Epidemics Method (MEM), Bai–Perron breaks, and lagged climate correlations]. Results The weighted circular peak month of infant bronchiolitis hospitalizations shifted after the pandemic in Costa (+2.16 months), Sierra (+1.22), and Amazonía (+1.91), attenuating with altitude from +2.19 months below 500 m to +0.88 at 1,500–3,000 m. Costa was stable across 2022–2024; Sierra and, more so, Amazonía varied widely year to year (Amazonía peak ranged March–July, n = 37–42/year), so the pooled estimate should be read with caution. The composite RSV-coded outcome tracked ICD-10 code J21's timing closely in Costa (0.15-month difference) and Sierra (0.40), supporting ICD-10 code J21 as a reasonable RSV proxy in both, and the three regional peaks now span 2 months. Conclusion Bronchiolitis seasonality in Ecuador changed after the pandemic, most clearly and stably in Costa; Sierra showed a transient 2022 perturbation with partial reversion by 2023–2024, and Amazonía's estimates remained unstable and underpowered. As region-tailored estimates are less stable in Sierra and Amazonía than in Costa, a single national window (nirsevimab February–April; maternal RSVpreF for pregnancies expected to deliver January–May) is a more defensible starting point, pending confirmation from further seasons and, ideally, laboratory-confirmed surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the lack of subnational evidence on bronchiolitis seasonality, altitudinal patterning, and post-pandemic stability in Ecuador to inform RSV immunoprophylaxis timing, given that acute bronchiolitis is mainly attributed to RSV and is the leading cause of infant hospitalization for lower respiratory tract disease worldwide.
Design and population
We conducted a national ecological time-series analysis of hospital discharges from the Ecuadorian Statistics Institute from 2007–2024, using data from 204 of 224 cantons with complete records, focusing on acute bronchiolitis (ICD-10 code J21) in children younger than 12 months (n = 23,190 nationally; 22,724 in the panel), with secondary outcomes across RSV-relevant age bands and RSV-specific ICD-10 codes.
Core methods
Analyses combined circular seasonality metrics with block-bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs), year-by-year disaggregation, and complementary sensitivity analyses including the Moving Epidemics Method (MEM), Bai–Perron breaks, and lagged climate correlations, with K35 (appendicitis) as a negative control.
Main results
The weighted circular peak month of infant bronchiolitis hospitalizations shifted after the pandemic in Costa (+2.16 months), Sierra (+1.22), and Amazonía (+1.91), with attenuation by altitude; Costa was stable across 2022–2024, while Sierra and Amazonía showed year-to-year variability, and the composite RSV-coded outcome closely tracked ICD-10 code J21's timing in Costa and Sierra.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that bronchiolitis seasonality in Ecuador changed post-pandemic, with the most clear and stable changes in Costa, while Sierra showed a transient perturbation and Amazonía's estimates remained unstable and underpowered, highlighting the novelty of regional and altitudinal heterogeneity in RSV seasonality.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that a single national window for nirsevimab (February–April) and maternal RSVpreF (pregnancies expected to deliver January–May) is a more defensible starting point for immunoprophylaxis timing, given the varying stability of region-tailored estimates, pending confirmation from further seasons and laboratory-confirmed surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to limitations including year-to-year variability in Sierra and Amazonía, unstable estimates in Amazonía, and the pooled estimate requiring caution; the study's ecological design and reliance on ICD-10 codes as RSV proxies also present potential limitations.

SMAD4–BPIFA1 轴控制气道 抗病毒药物 Myhre 综合征的防御

SMAD4–BPIFA1 Axis Governs Airway Antiviral Defense in Myhre Syndrome
作者： Yuanpu P. Di, Bodong Wen, Fengyuan Li, Felicia Tang, Dena Shen, Mark E. Lindsay, Angela E. Lin, Yin Chen, Hongmei Mou
摘要中文翻译Myhre 综合征是一种罕见的常染色体显性遗传疾病,由 SMAD4 的功能获得 (GOF) 变异引起。尽管它主要被认为是一种纤维化疾病,但我们和其他人已将呼吸道感染相关的发病率确定为主要的临床负担。无论如何,Myhre 综合征中病毒易感性增加的上皮内在机制仍然知之甚少。在这里,使用患者来源的鼻气道上皮细胞 (NEC) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染作为模型,我们证明,与匹配的健康对照相比,Myhre 综合征上皮细胞中的感染明显加剧。我们进一步表明,健康供体 NEC 表型中 TGF-β1-SMAD4 信号传导的激活增强了 RSV 易感性,将 SMAD4 信号传导确定为气道上皮中真正的前病毒途径。从机制上讲,我们发现 Myhre 综合征 NEC 中 BPIFA1 表达显着降低。BPIFA1 是一种关键的分泌型先天防御蛋白,具有抗菌、免疫调节和新兴的 抗病毒药物 功能。SMAD 信号传导抑制 BPIFA1 表达,其在健康细胞中的敲低模拟了病毒易感性的增加,将 SMAD4 功能获得与受损的上皮免疫联系起来。在治疗方面,我们评估了新型、稳定的 24 个氨基酸 BPIFA1 衍生肽作为潜在的干预措施。含有活性肽(A4-153 和 A4-X7)的 治疗 显着降低了健康和 Myhre 综合征气道上皮培养物中的 RSV 感染性和上皮损伤,而乱序对照肽(A4-198)则没有显示出显着效果。总的来说,我们的研究结果表明,GOF SMAD4 信号传导通过抑制 BPIFA1 介导的 抗病毒药物 防御来增强气道上皮的病毒脆弱性,并证明 BPIFA1 衍生的肽疗法代表了恢复先天 抗病毒药物 保护的有前途的策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Myhre 综合征是一种由功能获得性 SMAD4 变异引起的罕见常染色体显性遗传疾病,主要被认为是一种纤维化疾病,但具有显着的呼吸道感染相关发病率。该研究解决了关于 Myhre 综合征病毒易感性增加的上皮内在机制的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用患者来源的鼻气道上皮细胞 (NEC) 和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 感染作为模型,将 Myhre 综合征上皮细胞与匹配的健康对照进行比较。该研究评估了 TGF-β1–SMAD4 信号传导和 抗病毒药物 蛋白 BPIFA1 在气道上皮对 RSV 易感性中的作用。
核心方法
作者证明了 Myhre 综合征 NEC 中 RSV 感染相对于健康对照的严重程度,并表明健康供体 NEC 中 TGF-β1-SMAD4 的激活表型增强了 RSV 易感性。从机制上讲,他们通过抑制 BPIFA1 表达将 SMAD4 功能获得与受损的上皮免疫联系起来。
主要结果
与匹配的健康对照相比,Myhre 综合征上皮细胞中的 RSV 感染明显加剧。SMAD 信号传导抑制 BPIFA1 表达,并且其在健康细胞中的敲除增加了病毒易感性。治疗 与 BPIFA1 衍生肽(A4-153 和 A4-X7)显着降低 RSV 感染性和上皮损伤,而乱序对照肽(A4-198)则没有显示出显着效果。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,功能获得性 SMAD4 信号传导通过抑制 BPIFA1 介导的 抗病毒药物 防御来增强气道上皮的病毒脆弱性。该研究表明 BPIFA1 衍生的肽疗法代表了恢复先天 抗病毒药物 保护的有前途的策略。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,针对 SMAD4-BPIFA1 轴可以为减轻 Myhre 综合征个体 RSV 易感性的治疗策略提供参考。BPIFA1 衍生肽可能提供一种增强气道 抗病毒药物 防御的新方法。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其依赖于使用患者来源的 NEC 和 RSV 作为模型病原体的体外模型,这可能无法完全重现体内条件。由于缺乏体内验证且样本量较小,证据强度中等。

SMAD4–BPIFA1 Axis Governs Airway Antiviral Defense in Myhre Syndrome

Authors: Yuanpu P. Di, Bodong Wen, Fengyuan Li, Felicia Tang, Dena Shen, Mark E. Lindsay, Angela E. Lin, Yin Chen, Hongmei Mou
Original abstract (English)
Myhre syndrome is a rare autosomal-dominant disorder caused by gain-of-function (GOF) variants in SMAD4. Although it is primarily recognized as a fibrotic disease, we and others have identified respiratory infection-associated morbidity as a major clinical burden. Regardless, the epithelial-intrinsic mechanisms underlying increased viral susceptibility in Myhre syndrome remain poorly understood. Here, using patient-derived nasal airway epithelial cells (NECs) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection as a model, we demonstrate that infection is markedly exacerbated in Myhre syndrome epithelium relative to matched healthy controls. We further show that activation of TGF-β1–SMAD4 signaling in healthy donor NECs phenocopies augmented RSV susceptibility, identifying SMAD4 signaling as a bona fide pro-viral pathway in airway epithelium. Mechanistically, we identify a significant reduction in BPIFA1 expression in Myhre syndrome NECs. BPIFA1 is a key secreted innate defense protein with antimicrobial, immunomodulatory, and emerging antiviral functions. SMAD signaling suppresses BPIFA1 expression, and its knockdown in healthy cells mimics the increased viral susceptibility, linking SMAD4 gain-of-function to impaired epithelial immunity. Therapeutically, we evaluated novel, stabilized 24-amino acid BPIFA1-derived peptides as a potential intervention. Treatment with active peptides (A4-153 and A4-X7) significantly reduced RSV infectivity and epithelial damage in both healthy and Myhre syndrome airway epithelial cultures, whereas a scrambled control peptide (A4-198) showed no significant effect. Collectively, our findings establish that GOF SMAD4 signaling enhances viral vulnerability in airway epithelium through suppression of BPIFA1-mediated antiviral defense and demonstrate that BPIFA1-derived peptide therapeutics represent a promising strategy to restore innate antiviral protection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Myhre syndrome, a rare autosomal-dominant disorder caused by gain-of-function SMAD4 variants, is primarily recognized as a fibrotic disease but carries significant respiratory infection-associated morbidity. The study addresses the knowledge gap regarding epithelial-intrinsic mechanisms underlying increased viral susceptibility in Myhre syndrome.
Design and population
The study used patient-derived nasal airway epithelial cells (NECs) and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) infection as a model to compare Myhre syndrome epithelium with matched healthy controls. The research evaluated the role of TGF-β1–SMAD4 signaling and the antiviral protein BPIFA1 in airway epithelial susceptibility to RSV.
Core methods
The authors demonstrated RSV infection severity in Myhre syndrome NECs relative to healthy controls and showed that TGF-β1–SMAD4 activation in healthy donor NECs phenocopied augmented RSV susceptibility. Mechanistically, they linked SMAD4 gain-of-function to impaired epithelial immunity via suppression of BPIFA1 expression.
Main results
RSV infection was markedly exacerbated in Myhre syndrome epithelium compared to matched healthy controls. SMAD signaling suppressed BPIFA1 expression, and its knockdown in healthy cells increased viral susceptibility. Treatment with BPIFA1-derived peptides (A4-153 and A4-X7) significantly reduced RSV infectivity and epithelial damage, whereas a scrambled control peptide (A4-198) showed no significant effect.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings establish that gain-of-function SMAD4 signaling enhances viral vulnerability in airway epithelium by suppressing BPIFA1-mediated antiviral defense. The study demonstrates that BPIFA1-derived peptide therapeutics represent a promising strategy to restore innate antiviral protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that targeting the SMAD4–BPIFA1 axis could inform therapeutic strategies to mitigate RSV susceptibility in individuals with Myhre syndrome. The BPIFA1-derived peptides may offer a novel approach to enhance airway antiviral defense.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on in vitro models using patient-derived NECs and RSV as a model pathogen, which may not fully recapitulate in vivo conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the absence of in vivo validation and small sample size.

两种商用抗 RSV−IgG 和 IgA ELISA 在临床相关人群中的诊断性能和互换性

Diagnostic performance and interchangeability of two commercial anti-RSV−IgG and IgA ELISAs across clinically relevant populations
作者： Rainer Gosert, Svenia Schmid, Laura Infanti, Andreas Holbro, Andreas Buser, Jakob Passweg, Jörg Halter, Jonas Lötscher, Carla S. Walti, Sarah Tschudin-Sutter
摘要中文翻译摘要:随着 RSV 疫苗 和长效单克隆抗体预防的引入,准确评估抗 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 特异性抗体变得越来越重要。然而,市售 RSV-ELISA 的分析性能和互换性仍未得到充分定义,导致其临床诊断实践的可靠性存在不确定性。我们对广泛使用的商业 Euroimmun− 和 Virotech−ELISA 进行了头对头比较,用于检测来自四个 队列研究 的 462 份血清中的总抗 RSV−IgG 和 IgA:健康成人 (n = 100)、孕妇 (n = 59)、儿童 (n = 101) 和接种疫苗的移植受者 (n = 101;基线和天 + 30)。对融合前 F (pre-F) 特异性 IgG 进行定量,以推断功能相关的抗体反应。评估了定性一致性、定量偏差、相关性和年龄依赖性模式。检测之间抗 RSV-IgG 检测的定性一致性很高 (83.8%)。然而,Virotech-ELISA 系统地产生了更高的 IgG 检测率和定量值 (P s = 0.92) 和接种疫苗的移植受者 (rs = 0.79)。抗 RSV-IgA 很少被检测到 (6%–11%),检测间一致性中等 (79.4%)。健康成人和孕妇的 IgG-IgA 相关性较弱 (r s s ≥ 0.40)。Euroimmun− 和 Virotech−ELISA 在分析上不可互换,并且表现出系统分析依赖性差异。对临床相关人群中总抗 RSV-IgG、前 F-IgG 和 IgA 的综合分析强调需要进行分析和人群感知的解释,以支持 RSV 血清学 在血清流行病学研究和诊断实验室实践中的可靠使用。重要性 随着最近呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗 和婴儿单克隆抗体预防的推出,血清学检测在 疫苗 免疫原性试验、许可后评估和基于人群的血清监测中变得越来越重要。尽管商业 ELISA 具有协调抗体测试的潜力,但其适当的应用需要详细了解特定检测的性能和校准特性。验证不充分可能会限制实验室间的可比性,使得相同的样品在各个中心产生不同的定量结果,从而使多中心 监测 和 疫苗 研究中的纵向抗体监测和协调分析变得复杂。在这种情况下,本研究对两种广泛使用的 RSV-ELISA 进行了系统的交叉比较,并表明,尽管具有良好的定性一致性,但它们在定量上不可互换,并且在不同人群和免疫背景下的 IgG 和 IgA 测量中表现出系统差异。通过表征不同临床环境中总 IgG、前 F-特异性 IgG 和 IgA 的性能,这项工作支持临床诊断实践中 RSV 血清学 的明智检测选择和解释。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
随着 RSV 疫苗 和长效单克隆抗体预防的引入,抗 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 特异性抗体的准确评估变得越来越重要,但市售 RSV-ELISA 的分析性能和互换性仍然没有充分定义。
设计与对象
我们对广泛使用的商业 Euroimmun− 和 Virotech−ELISA 进行了头对头比较,用于检测来自四个 队列研究 的 462 份血清中的总抗 RSV−IgG 和 IgA:健康成人 (n = 100)、孕妇 (n = 59)、儿童 (n = 101) 和接种疫苗的移植受者 (n = 101;基线和天 + 30)。
核心方法
评估了定性一致性、定量偏差、相关性和年龄依赖性模式,并对前 F 特异性 IgG 进行定量以推断功能相关的抗体反应。
主要结果
检测之间抗 RSV-IgG 检测的定性一致性很高 (83.8%),但 Virotech-ELISA 系统地产生了更高的 IgG 检测率和定量值 (P < 0.001),具有中度至强相关性(健康成人中 r s = 0.92;接种疫苗的移植受者中 r s = 0.79)。抗 RSV-IgA 很少被检测到 (6%–11%),检测间一致性中等 (79.4%)。
解释与创新
Euroimmun− 和 Virotech−ELISA 在分析上不可互换,并表现出系统性检测依赖性差异,综合分析强调需要进行检测和群体感知解释,以支持 RSV 血清学 的可靠使用。
科研与公卫意义
这项工作通过表征不同临床环境中的性能,支持在临床诊断实践、血清流行病学研究和 疫苗 评估中对 RSV 血清学 进行明智的检测选择和解释。
局限与证据强度
该研究表明,检测之间具有高度的定性一致性,但存在显着的定量差异,由于对多个临床相关人群和免疫环境进行系统的交叉比较,证据强度被评估为高。

Diagnostic performance and interchangeability of two commercial anti-RSV−IgG and IgA ELISAs across clinically relevant populations

Authors: Rainer Gosert, Svenia Schmid, Laura Infanti, Andreas Holbro, Andreas Buser, Jakob Passweg, Jörg Halter, Jonas Lötscher, Carla S. Walti, Sarah Tschudin-Sutter
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Accurate assessment of anti-respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-specific antibodies has gained importance following the introduction of RSV vaccines and long-acting monoclonal antibody prophylaxis. However, the analytical performance and interchangeability of commercially available RSV−ELISAs remain insufficiently defined, leaving uncertainty about their reliability for clinical diagnostic practice. We performed a head-to-head comparison of the widely used commercial Euroimmun− and Virotech‒ELISA for detection of total anti-RSV‒IgG and IgA in 462 sera from four cohorts: healthy adults ( n = 100), pregnant women ( n = 59), children ( n = 101), and vaccinated transplant recipients ( n = 101; baseline and day + 30). Prefusion-F (pre-F)-specific IgG was quantified to infer functionally relevant antibody responses. Qualitative agreement, quantitative bias, correlations, and age-dependent patterns were assessed. Qualitative concordance in anti-RSV‒IgG detection was high (83.8%) between assays. However, the Virotech‒ELISA systematically yielded higher IgG detection rates and quantitative values ( P s = 0.92) and vaccinated transplant recipients (r s = 0.79). Anti-RSV‒IgA was detected infrequently (6%–11%), with moderate (79.4%) inter-assay concordance. IgG‒IgA correlations were weak in healthy adults and pregnant women (r s s ≥ 0.40). The Euroimmun− and Virotech‒ELISAs are not analytically interchangeable and exhibit systematic assay-dependent differences. Integrated analysis of total anti-RSV‒IgG, pre-F‒IgG, and IgA across clinically relevant populations emphasizes the need for assay- and population-aware interpretation to support reliable use of RSV serology in seroepidemiological studies and diagnostic laboratory practice. IMPORTANCE With the recent introduction of espiratory syncytial virus (RSV) vaccines and infant monoclonal antibody prophylaxis, serological testing is gaining relevance in vaccine immunogenicity trials, post-licensure evaluations, and population-based serosurveillance. Although commercial ELISAs offer the potential to harmonize antibody testing, their appropriate application requires a detailed understanding of assay-specific performance and calibration characteristics. Inadequate validation can limit inter-laboratory comparability, such that identical samples yield different quantitative results across centers, thereby complicating longitudinal antibody monitoring and harmonized analysis in multicenter surveillance and vaccine studies. In this context, the present study provides a systematic cross-comparison of two widely used RSV−ELISAs and demonstrates that, despite good qualitative agreement, they are not quantitatively interchangeable and exhibit systematic differences in IgG and IgA measurements across populations and immune contexts. By characterizing the performance of total IgG, pre-F−specific IgG, and IgA in diverse clinical settings, this work supports informed assay selection and interpretation of RSV serology in clinical diagnostic practice.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Accurate assessment of anti-respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-specific antibodies has gained importance following the introduction of RSV vaccines and long-acting monoclonal antibody prophylaxis, but the analytical performance and interchangeability of commercially available RSV−ELISAs remain insufficiently defined.
Design and population
We performed a head-to-head comparison of the widely used commercial Euroimmun− and Virotech‒ELISA for detection of total anti-RSV‒IgG and IgA in 462 sera from four cohorts: healthy adults (n = 100), pregnant women (n = 59), children (n = 101), and vaccinated transplant recipients (n = 101; baseline and day + 30).
Core methods
Qualitative agreement, quantitative bias, correlations, and age-dependent patterns were assessed, with pre-F-specific IgG quantified to infer functionally relevant antibody responses.
Main results
Qualitative concordance in anti-RSV‒IgG detection was high (83.8%) between assays, but the Virotech‒ELISA systematically yielded higher IgG detection rates and quantitative values (P < 0.001), with moderate to strong correlations (r s = 0.92 in healthy adults; r s = 0.79 in vaccinated transplant recipients). Anti-RSV‒IgA was detected infrequently (6%–11%), with moderate (79.4%) inter-assay concordance.
Interpretation and novelty
The Euroimmun− and Virotech‒ELISAs are not analytically interchangeable and exhibit systematic assay-dependent differences, with integrated analysis emphasizing the need for assay- and population-aware interpretation to support reliable use of RSV serology.
Scientific and public-health significance
This work supports informed assay selection and interpretation of RSV serology in clinical diagnostic practice, seroepidemiological studies, and vaccine evaluations by characterizing performance across diverse clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates high qualitative agreement but significant quantitative differences between assays, with evidence strength assessed as high due to systematic cross-comparison across multiple clinically relevant populations and immune contexts.

SARS-CoV-2 RBD 融合可改善 BALB/c 小鼠中 RSV G CCD mRNA 疫苗 诱导的增强疾病

SARS-CoV-2 RBD fusion ameliorates enhanced disease induced by RSV G CCD mRNA vaccine in BALB/c mice
作者： Yufang Sun, Ying Xie, Lian Liu, Huijie Yang, Jiaojiao Nie, Wenwen Wang, Su Zhang, Yaru Quan, Chang-Gui Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是导致婴儿和老年人下呼吸道感染的主要病原体。然而,疫苗 的发展一直受到 疫苗 增强疾病 (VED) 风险的阻碍,而 RSV 附着 (G) 糖蛋白的作用长期以来一直被忽视。本研究使用 mRNA 疫苗 平台,系统评估了两个关键 RSV G 蛋白结构域(中央保守结构域 (CCD) 和粘蛋白样结构域)在介导免疫保护和 VED 中的作用。结果表明,粘蛋白样结构域不提供保护并诱导显着的 VED。令人惊讶的是,尽管 CCD 免疫引发了有效的体液免疫并显着降低了病毒载量,但它也会诱导 Th2 偏向反应并加剧 RSV 攻击后的肺部炎症。然而,CCD 与 SARS-CoV-2 的受体结合域 (RBD) 在 mRNA 疫苗 (CCD + RBD) 中的融合成功地将 Th2 偏向的 免疫应答 重定向到平衡的 Th1/Th2 谱,同时显着减弱 CCD 诱导的免疫病理学。CD8+ T 细胞耗竭和过继转移实验进一步支持了 RBD 反应性 CD8+ T 细胞对病理学减少的贡献。这些发现提供了概念证明,即异源抗原融合可以在 BALB/c RSV 攻击模型中重塑 CCD 相关的免疫病理学。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是导致婴儿和老年人下呼吸道感染的主要病原体,但 疫苗 的发展一直受到 疫苗 增强疾病 (VED) 风险的阻碍,而 RSV 附着 (G) 糖蛋白的作用长期以来一直被忽视。
设计与对象
本研究使用 mRNA 疫苗 平台,系统评估了两个关键 RSV G 蛋白结构域(中央保守结构域 (CCD) 和粘蛋白样结构域)在介导 BALB/c 小鼠免疫保护和 VED 中的作用。
核心方法
该研究采用针对特定 RSV G 蛋白结构域(包括 CCD 和粘蛋白样结构域)的 mRNA 疫苗,并评估了 BALB/c 小鼠 RSV 攻击后的 免疫应答 和病理学。
主要结果
结果表明,粘蛋白样结构域不提供保护并诱导显着的 VED;CCD 免疫引发有效的体液免疫并显着降低病毒载量,但也会诱导 Th2 偏向反应并加剧 RSV 攻击后的肺部炎症。
解释与创新
作者解释说,CCD 与 SARS-CoV-2 的受体结合域 (RBD) 在 mRNA 疫苗 (CCD + RBD) 中的融合成功地将 Th2 偏向的 免疫应答 重定向到平衡的 Th1/Th2 谱,同时显着减弱 CCD 诱导的免疫病理学,这提供了异源抗原融合可以重塑 CCD 相关免疫病理学的概念证明。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,异源抗原融合策略可以减轻 疫苗 增强的 RSV 疫苗 疾病风险,为未来的 疫苗 设计和开发工作提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在 BALB/c 小鼠模型中进行的,这可能限制了对人类的推广;然而,由于对特定领域的系统评估和对照实验的使用,证据强度很高。

SARS-CoV-2 RBD fusion ameliorates enhanced disease induced by RSV G CCD mRNA vaccine in BALB/c mice

Authors: Yufang Sun, Ying Xie, Lian Liu, Huijie Yang, Jiaojiao Nie, Wenwen Wang, Su Zhang, Yaru Quan, Chang-Gui Li
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a primary pathogen responsible for lower respiratory tract infections in both infants and the elderly. However, vaccine development has been consistently hampered by the risk of vaccine-enhanced disease (VED), while the role of the RSV attachment (G) glycoprotein has long been overlooked. Using an mRNA vaccine platform, this study systematically evaluated the roles of two critical RSV G protein domains—the central conserved domain (CCD) and the mucin-like domains—in mediating immune protection and VED. Results indicate that the mucin-like domains confer no protection and induce marked VED. Surprisingly, although CCD immunization elicits potent humoral immunity and significantly reduces viral load, it also induces Th2-biased responses and exacerbates pulmonary inflammation following RSV challenge. However, fusion of CCD with the receptor-binding domain (RBD) of SARS-CoV-2 in an mRNA vaccine (CCD + RBD) successfully redirected the Th2-biased immune responses toward a balanced Th1/Th2 profile, while significantly attenuating CCD-induced immunopathology. CD8+ T-cell depletion and adoptive-transfer experiments further supported a contribution of RBD-responsive CD8+ T cells to the reduction in pathology. These findings provide proof of concept that heterologous antigen fusion can reshape CCD-associated immunopathology in a BALB/c RSV challenge model.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a primary pathogen responsible for lower respiratory tract infections in both infants and the elderly, but vaccine development has been consistently hampered by the risk of vaccine-enhanced disease (VED), while the role of the RSV attachment (G) glycoprotein has long been overlooked.
Design and population
Using an mRNA vaccine platform, this study systematically evaluated the roles of two critical RSV G protein domains—the central conserved domain (CCD) and the mucin-like domains—in mediating immune protection and VED in BALB/c mice.
Core methods
The study employed mRNA vaccines targeting specific RSV G protein domains, including CCD and mucin-like domains, and evaluated immune responses and pathology following RSV challenge in BALB/c mice.
Main results
Results indicate that the mucin-like domains confer no protection and induce marked VED; CCD immunization elicits potent humoral immunity and significantly reduces viral load but also induces Th2-biased responses and exacerbates pulmonary inflammation following RSV challenge.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that fusion of CCD with the receptor-binding domain (RBD) of SARS-CoV-2 in an mRNA vaccine (CCD + RBD) successfully redirected the Th2-biased immune responses toward a balanced Th1/Th2 profile while significantly attenuating CCD-induced immunopathology, providing proof of concept that heterologous antigen fusion can reshape CCD-associated immunopathology.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that heterologous antigen fusion strategies could mitigate vaccine-enhanced disease risks for RSV vaccines, informing future vaccine design and development efforts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a BALB/c mouse model, which may limit generalizability to humans; however, the evidence strength is high due to the systematic evaluation of specific domains and the use of controlled experiments.

用于大规模呼吸道病毒测序的诱饵捕获宏基因组学的基准测试和优化

Benchmarking and optimisation of bait-capture metagenomics for sequencing of respiratory viruses at scale
作者： Josef Wagner, Diana Rajan, Sarah Frances Field, Emma Betteridge, Katie Bellis, Marissa Knoll, Duncan Y. K. Ng, Diego Teixeira, Beth Blane, Asha Akram
摘要中文翻译对呼吸道病毒 基因组 进行测序对于公共卫生和研究至关重要。尽管鸟枪法宏基因组学与病原体无关,但其敏感性受到大量脱靶 宿主 核酸的限制。杂交诱饵捕获通过在测序前选择性富集病毒序列来克服这一限制。我们评估了三种呼吸道病毒诱饵捕获工作流程(veSEQ、RVI-seq 和 Illumina),以比较它们的性能并评估它们对可扩展呼吸道病毒测序的适用性。对含有 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感、人副流感病毒和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 的合成 RNA 对照和临床样本进行了分析。所有三个工作流程在呼吸道病毒中都表现出高效率、可重复性和广泛可比的性能。完整的病毒 基因组 始终从含有 10,000 个病毒拷贝的样本中回收,而病毒读数在病毒载量大幅降低(包括大约 100 个拷贝)的情况下仍然可检测到。工作流程优化降低了试剂成本并实现了实验室自动化,同时又不影响测序灵敏度。杂交诱饵捕获为呼吸道病毒 基因组 测序提供了一种有效且可扩展的方法。可以在不影响性能的情况下实现成本降低和自动化,支持其在常规 基因组监测 和公共卫生准备中的使用。

对呼吸道病毒 基因组 进行测序对于公共卫生和研究至关重要。

核心方法

所有三个工作流程在呼吸道病毒中都表现出高效率、可重复性和广泛可比的性能。完整的病毒 基因组 始终从含有 10,000 个病毒拷贝的样本中回收,而病毒读数在病毒载量大幅降低(包括大约 100 个拷贝)的情况下仍然可检测到。

科研与公卫意义
可以在不影响性能的情况下实现成本降低和自动化,支持其在常规 基因组监测 和公共卫生准备中的使用。
局限与证据强度
尽管鸟枪法宏基因组学与病原体无关,但其敏感性受到大量脱靶 宿主 核酸的限制。由于对多个工作流程的综合评估以及跨病毒载量的一致结果,证据强度很高。

Benchmarking and optimisation of bait-capture metagenomics for sequencing of respiratory viruses at scale

Authors: Josef Wagner, Diana Rajan, Sarah Frances Field, Emma Betteridge, Katie Bellis, Marissa Knoll, Duncan Y. K. Ng, Diego Teixeira, Beth Blane, Asha Akram
Original abstract (English)
Sequencing respiratory virus genomes is essential for public health surveillance and research. Although shotgun metagenomics is pathogen agnostic, its sensitivity is limited by abundant off-target host nucleic acids. Hybridization bait capture overcomes this limitation by selectively enriching viral sequences prior to sequencing. We evaluated three respiratory viral bait capture workflows (veSEQ, RVI-seq, and Illumina) to compare their performance and assess their suitability for scalable respiratory virus sequencing. Synthetic RNA controls and clinical samples containing SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, human parainfluenza virus, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were analysed. All three workflows demonstrated high efficiency, reproducibility and broadly comparable performance across respiratory viruses. Complete viral genomes were consistently recovered from samples containing 10,000 viral copies, while viral reads remained detectable at substantially lower viral loads, including approximately 100 copies. Workflow optimisation reduced reagent costs and enabled laboratory automation without compromising sequencing sensitivity. Hybridization bait capture provides an effective and scalable approach for respiratory virus genome sequencing. Cost reductions and automation can be implemented without compromising performance, supporting its use in routine genomic surveillance and public health preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Sequencing respiratory virus genomes is essential for public health surveillance and research.
Design and population
We evaluated three respiratory viral bait capture workflows (veSEQ, RVI-seq, and Illumina) to compare their performance and assess their suitability for scalable respiratory virus sequencing.
Core methods
Hybridization bait capture overcomes this limitation by selectively enriching viral sequences prior to sequencing. Synthetic RNA controls and clinical samples containing SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, human parainfluenza virus, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) were analysed. Workflow optimisation reduced reagent costs and enabled laboratory automation without compromising sequencing sensitivity.
Main results
All three workflows demonstrated high efficiency, reproducibility and broadly comparable performance across respiratory viruses. Complete viral genomes were consistently recovered from samples containing 10,000 viral copies, while viral reads remained detectable at substantially lower viral loads, including approximately 100 copies.
Interpretation and novelty
Hybridization bait capture provides an effective and scalable approach for respiratory virus genome sequencing.
Scientific and public-health significance
Cost reductions and automation can be implemented without compromising performance, supporting its use in routine genomic surveillance and public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
Although shotgun metagenomics is pathogen agnostic, its sensitivity is limited by abundant off-target host nucleic acids. The evidence strength is high due to the comprehensive evaluation of multiple workflows and consistent results across viral loads.

RSV 塑造儿童血清型特异性侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病动态:一项观察性研究

RSV shapes serotype-specific invasive pneumococcal disease dynamics in children: an observational study
作者： Inès Fafi, Corinne Levy, Emmanuelle Varon, Robert Cohen, Stéphane Béchet, Zaba Valtuille, André Birgy, Stéphane Bonacorsi, Jean-François Delobbe, Christophe Batard
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 肺炎球菌鼻咽定植是侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病 (IPD) 的第一步。呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 可能引发 IPD,特别是对于疾病潜力较低的血清型。我们的目的是根据 RSV 循环水平分析儿童 IPD 中肺炎球菌血清型的分布。方法 我们使用了法国 监测 三个连续系统 2008 年至 2023 年关于 IPD、肺炎球菌携带和 RSV 感染的数据。根据 RSV 流行阈值,我们根据“RSV 季节”和“非 RSV 季节”评估了 IPD 中每种血清型的比例。我们根据非 RSV 季节与 RSV 季节评估了血清型特异性参与 IPD 的可能性与每种血清型的估计疾病潜力之间的相关性。结果 在 4,574 例 IPD 病例中,非 RSV 季节与 RSV 季节期间的血清型分布差异很大。血清型的疾病潜力越低,RSV 循环就越能增强其对 IPD 的参与(Rho=0.60,CI95% 0.28 至 0.80,p 值 = 0.0012)。结论 RSV 在引发 IPD 中的作用在不同肺炎球菌血清型中存在很大差异。应根据这些数据评估针对 RSV 的干预措施的影响。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
肺炎球菌鼻咽定植先于侵袭性肺炎球菌疾病 (IPD),呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 可能引发 IPD,特别是对于疾病潜力较低的血清型。该研究解决了有关 RSV 循环如何影响导致儿童 IPD 的肺炎球菌血清型分布的知识差距。
设计与对象
该研究使用了法国 监测 三个连续系统在 2008 年至 2023 年间收集的关于 IPD、肺炎球菌携带和 RSV 感染的数据。它比较了 RSV 季节与非 RSV 季节儿童 IPD 病例中每种肺炎球菌血清型的比例。
核心方法
该分析通过比较非 RSV 季节与 RSV 季节数据,评估了 RSV 循环水平与 IPD 中血清型特异性参与的可能性之间的相关性。估计了血清型特异性疾病的可能性,并将其与 RSV 循环期间参与的变化相关联。
主要结果
在 4,574 例 IPD 病例中,非 RSV 季节和 RSV 季节之间的血清型分布存在显着差异。血清型的疾病潜力越低,RSV 循环就越能增强其对 IPD 的参与(Rho=0.60,CI95% 0.28 至 0.80,p 值 = 0.0012)。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,RSV 在引发 IPD 中的作用在不同肺炎球菌血清型中存在很大差异,这凸显了之前未被充分认识的血清型特异性相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,针对 RSV 的干预措施可能需要考虑 IPD 风险中血清型特异性动态,为未来的 疫苗接种 和 监测 策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察设计和对 监测 数据的依赖而受到限制,这可能会引入偏差并限制因果推断。由于缺乏实验对照和潜在的未测量的混杂因素,证据强度中等。

RSV shapes serotype-specific invasive pneumococcal disease dynamics in children: an observational study

Authors: Inès Fafi, Corinne Levy, Emmanuelle Varon, Robert Cohen, Stéphane Béchet, Zaba Valtuille, André Birgy, Stéphane Bonacorsi, Jean-François Delobbe, Christophe Batard
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Pneumococcal nasopharyngeal colonization is the first step toward invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD). Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) may trigger IPD, particularly for serotypes with low disease potential. We aimed to analyse the distribution of pneumococcal serotypes among paediatric IPD according to RSV circulation level. Methods We used data from three continuous French surveillance systems on IPD, pneumococcal carriage and RSV infections from 2008 to 2023. Based on the RSV epidemics threshold, we assessed the proportion of each serotype among IPD, depending on “RSV season” and “non-RSV season”. We assessed the correlation between the likelihood of serotype-specific involvement in IPD according to non-RSV season vs RSV season and the estimated disease potential of each serotype. Results Among 4,574 IPD cases, the serotype distribution strongly varied during non-RSV season vs RSV season. The lower the disease potential of serotypes, the more RSV circulation enhanced their involvement in IPD (Rho=0.60, CI95% 0.28 to 0.80, p-value = 0.0012). Conclusions The role of RSV in triggering IPD strongly varies across pneumococcal serotypes. The impact of RSV-targeted interventions should be evaluated in light of these data.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Pneumococcal nasopharyngeal colonization precedes invasive pneumococcal disease (IPD), and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) may trigger IPD, particularly for serotypes with low disease potential. The study addressed a knowledge gap regarding how RSV circulation influences the distribution of pneumococcal serotypes causing IPD in children.
Design and population
The study used data from three continuous French surveillance systems on IPD, pneumococcal carriage, and RSV infections collected between 2008 and 2023. It compared the proportion of each pneumococcal serotype among paediatric IPD cases during RSV seasons versus non-RSV seasons.
Core methods
The analysis assessed the correlation between RSV circulation level and the likelihood of serotype-specific involvement in IPD by comparing non-RSV season versus RSV season data. Serotype-specific disease potential was estimated and correlated with changes in involvement during RSV circulation.
Main results
Among 4,574 IPD cases, the serotype distribution varied significantly between non-RSV and RSV seasons. The lower the disease potential of serotypes, the more RSV circulation enhanced their involvement in IPD (Rho=0.60, CI95% 0.28 to 0.80, p-value = 0.0012).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that RSV’s role in triggering IPD varies substantially across pneumococcal serotypes, highlighting serotype-specific interactions that were previously underappreciated.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that RSV-targeted interventions may need to account for serotype-specific dynamics in IPD risk, informing future vaccination and surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design and reliance on surveillance data, which may introduce biases and limit causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of experimental controls and potential unmeasured confounders.

AS01 E -佐剂 呼吸道合胞病毒 预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗(佐剂 RSVPreF3):上市后前 2 年安全性回顾 监测。

AS01 E -Adjuvanted Respiratory Syncytial Virus Prefusion F Protein Vaccine (Adjuvanted RSVPreF3): Review of the First 2 Years of Post-Marketing Safety Surveillance.
作者： Catherine Tregunno, Quentin Deraedt, Susanna Cuadripani, Céline Verheust, Philip Bryan
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述重点关注 AS01E 佐剂 呼吸道合胞病毒 预融合 F 蛋白 疫苗(佐剂 RSVPreF3)用于预防 60 岁以上个体 呼吸道合胞病毒 下呼吸道疾病的上市后安全性。
证据基础与方法
The review analyzed spontaneous adverse events reported worldwide from the first marketing authorization (3 May 2023) to 2 May 2025, using the GSK global safety database. A total of 13.4 million doses were distributed globally, and 2861 spontaneous reports, including 7012 adverse events (11.0% serious), were received.
共识与结论
佐剂 RSVPreF3 的安全性与临床试验中观察到的一致,显示出未改变且有利的获益-风险特征。大多数不良事件是非严重的反应原事件,观察到的吉兰-巴利综合征和心房颤动的发生率并不表明与背景 发病率 相比风险过高。
争议与缺口
虽然大多数 疫苗接种 错误(例如,错误的产品管理或不适当的年龄)与不良事件无关,但正在进行 监测 来评估潜在的罕见不良事件,如吉兰-巴利综合征。这些数据反映了现实世界的使用情况,但由于自发报告的限制,可能无法捕获所有罕见事件。
科研与实践启示
持续上市后 监测 对于监测罕见不良事件和进一步评估 疫苗 的长期安全性至关重要。现实世界的数据支持 疫苗 在授权人群中的使用,但建议对来自常规医疗实践的安全信息进行持续分析,以识别任何潜在的新风险。

AS01 E -Adjuvanted Respiratory Syncytial Virus Prefusion F Protein Vaccine (Adjuvanted RSVPreF3): Review of the First 2 Years of Post-Marketing Safety Surveillance.

Authors: Catherine Tregunno, Quentin Deraedt, Susanna Cuadripani, Céline Verheust, Philip Bryan
Original abstract (English)
The AS01E-adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus prefusion F protein vaccine (adjuvanted RSVPreF3) received its first marketing authorization in May 2023 for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus lower respiratory tract disease in individuals aged ≥ 60 years. We conducted a review of post-marketing safety surveillance data following adjuvanted RSVPreF3 administration. Spontaneous adverse events (AEs) that were reported worldwide since the first approval (3 May, 2023) to 2 May, 2025 in the GSK global safety database were analyzed. Up to 2 May, 2025, 13.4 million doses of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 were distributed globally. A total of 2861 spontaneous reports, including 7012 AEs (serious: 11.0%), were received. The most frequent AEs consisted mainly of non-serious reactogenic events. Of all reports, 1164 described vaccination errors, with most frequent errors consisting of either wrong product administration (N = 243) or administration to patients of an inappropriate age (N = 204). The majority (90.3%) of vaccination error reports were not associated with any other AEs. Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 was erroneously administered to pregnant individuals in 415 cases; most reports (95.2%) occurred without associated AEs. Observed-to-expected analysis of reports of Guillain–Barré syndrome (N = 37) did not suggest an increased risk compared to background incidence of this event and the reports of atrial fibrillation (N = 29) did not indicate an excess risk. Review of the post-marketing surveillance data from 2 years of exposure to adjuvanted RSVPreF3 showed that the safety profile of the vaccine is consistent with that observed in clinical trials supporting an unchanged and favorable benefit-risk profile for the vaccine. Additional post-marketing surveillance is ongoing to further evaluate a potential risk of rare AEs, such as Guillain–Barré syndrome, following adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccination. Respiratory syncytial virus is an important cause of severe lung infections in older adults. Adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine was first approved in 2023 to prevent respiratory syncytial virus disease in adults aged 60 years and older. Regularly analyzing safety information from ongoing studies and real-world data (from routine healthcare practice) is essential to identify any potential new risks linked to adjuvanted RSVPreF3. Using the manufacturer’s (GSK) safety database, we analyzed the real-world safety data from individuals who experienced adverse events after vaccination with adjuvanted RSVPreF3. These data were collected during the first 2 years since the vaccine’s initial approval. The majority of adverse events reported to the manufacturer were mild and consistent with those reported in clinical trials. Most individuals who received the vaccine by mistake did not experience adverse events. The proportion of individuals who experienced Guillain–Barré syndrome after vaccination was within the range of background incidence rates of this disease in older adults. The reports of atrial fibrillation reflected the natural history of the disease for the older population. The safety data, collected over 2 years of substantial exposure to adjuvanted RSVPreF3, show that the safety profile of this vaccine is similar to that seen in clinical trials. Together with pre-licensure data demonstrating the vaccine’s ability to protect and help the immune system against respiratory syncytial virus disease, and real-world data demonstrating that adjuvanted RSVPreF3 protects against respiratory syncytial virus-associated hospitalization, the safety information collected so far supports the vaccine’s use for the people for whom it is authorized.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This review focuses on the post-marketing safety surveillance of the AS01E-adjuvanted respiratory syncytial virus prefusion F protein vaccine (adjuvanted RSVPreF3) for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus lower respiratory tract disease in individuals aged ≥ 60 years.
Evidence base and method
The review analyzed spontaneous adverse events reported worldwide from the first marketing authorization (3 May 2023) to 2 May 2025, using the GSK global safety database. A total of 13.4 million doses were distributed globally, and 2861 spontaneous reports, including 7012 adverse events (11.0% serious), were received.
Consensus and conclusions
The safety profile of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 is consistent with that observed in clinical trials, showing an unchanged and favorable benefit-risk profile. Most adverse events were non-serious reactogenic events, and observed rates of Guillain–Barré syndrome and atrial fibrillation did not indicate excess risk compared to background incidence.
Controversies and gaps
While the majority of vaccination errors (e.g., wrong product administration or inappropriate age) were not associated with adverse events, there is ongoing surveillance to evaluate potential rare adverse events like Guillain–Barré syndrome. The data reflect real-world use but may not capture all rare events due to spontaneous reporting limitations.
Research and practice implications
Continued post-marketing surveillance is essential to monitor rare adverse events and further assess the vaccine's long-term safety. The real-world data support the vaccine's use for authorized populations, but ongoing analysis of safety information from routine healthcare practice is recommended to identify any potential new risks.
